Any Analyst may edit any other Analyst's draft\n(shift continuity), though the original author is preserved.\n_Avoid_: Editor, intern (interns are Analysts, not a separate role), staff\n\n**Data Bite**:\nA Post: a short, dated item — a survey result, an enrollment snapshot, an\nannouncement. The one-way gate: a Report only crosses\nit with Director action.\n_Avoid_: Live, public\n\n**Factbook**:\nA specific Report: the office's running reference document of admissions,\nenrollment, diversity, and academic program statistics.\n\n**Common Data Set (CDS)**:\nA specific Report: a standardized annual institutional data submission,\npublished publicly.\n\n**Survey Calendar**:\nA specific Report: the schedule of past, current, and upcoming surveys the\noffice runs (e.g. faculty/staff surveys, external review surveys).\n-----\n4b4d114 T03: Data Bites — create, edit, publish, unpublish, delete\nb9fcf90 docs: glossary, ADR-003, client brief, field notes, usage ledger, transcripts\nca20662 T02: User management — create, change role, deactivate\nd4d3707 T01: Auth — login, logout, sessions","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8ec8eb31-8822-4e02-b610-28a3f6ebd6f8","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"edcc246d-fecf-4edb-94b4-7bdab820f6f4","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"04e24caf-e76d-4ac6-b3f7-74cb97b3f994","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XkbBi8Wo9ZG7bGmJtEQQGo","type":"tool_result","content":"T04: Reports (draft/publish lockdown)\n## Parent\n\nPart of #1\n\n## What to build\n\nAnalyst can create and edit a draft Report (title, slug, required Markdown body). Once published, only Director can edit, unpublish, delete, or publish it. Slug uniqueness is enforced within Reports, independently of Data Bites (the same slug may be reused across the two content types, per ADR-003's export-path namespacing).\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] Analyst can create a draft Report with a required Markdown body\n- [ ] Analyst can edit a draft Report, including one another Analyst started\n- [ ] Analyst gets 403/redirect attempting to publish a Report\n- [ ] Once published, Analyst gets 403/redirect attempting to edit, unpublish, or delete it\n- [ ] Director can publish, edit, unpublish, and delete a Report in any state\n- [ ] A second Report with a duplicate slug is rejected with a validation error; a Data Bite may share a slug with a Report without conflict\n- [ ] A draft Report is never returned by any public-facing query\n- [ ] All state-changing POSTs require CSRF\n- [ ] Tests cover all of the above\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- #2 (T01)\n-----SPEC\n## Problem Statement\n\nKenyon's Office of Institutional Research (Director + rotating student Analysts) currently publishes Data Bites and Reports by emailing files around and dropping them in a shared OneDrive folder. With multiple interns working at once, nobody can tell which version is current, and people outside the office resort to emailing IR directly to ask for the latest Factbook instead of finding it themselves.\n\n## Solution\n\nA CMS with two roles — Director (admin) and Analyst (editor; each intern gets their own named account) — that separates draft from published content. Analysts can draft, edit, publish, and unpublish Data Bites (short, dated Markdown items) without waiting on the Director. Reports (Factbook, Common Data Set, Survey Policy) are Markdown pages, same as Data Bites, with an optional attached file (e.g. the actual Factbook PDF, when one exists) — matching how these documents actually circulate while keeping the core rubric's required Markdown-body field intact. Only the Director can publish, edit, unpublish, or delete a Report once it exists. `cms publish` exports only published content to a static public site on GitHub Pages, with content-type-namespaced paths.\n\n## User Stories\n\n1. As a Director, I want to log in with a password, so that I can access the admin console.\n2. As an Analyst, I want to log in with my own account, so that whichever intern is on shift can post routine content under their own name.\n3. As anyone, I want a wrong password rejected with a generic message, so that the login form doesn't reveal whether an account exists.\n4. As any authenticated user (Director or Analyst), I want to log out, so that a shared machine doesn't stay signed in.\n5. As a Director, I want to create a new user account (Director or Analyst), so that I can grant access as staffing changes.\n6. As a Director, I want to change a user's role, so that I can correct access if someone's responsibilities change.\n7. As a Director, I want to deactivate a user, so that someone who's left the office can't log in anymore — even with a browser tab still holding an old session cookie — without deleting their history.\n8. As an Analyst, I want the admin console to hide user management entirely, so that I'm not tempted or able to touch accounts.\n9. As an Analyst, I want to create a Data Bite (title, slug, Markdown body), so that I can post a survey result or announcement.\n10. As an Analyst, I want to save a Data Bite as a draft, so that I can work on it across multiple sessions before it's ready.\n11. As an Analyst, I want to publish a Data Bite myself, so that I don't have to wait on the Director for routine content.\n12. As an Analyst, I want to edit or unpublish a Data Bite (including one another Analyst drafted or published), so that shift changes don't block fixing a mistake.\n13. As a Director, I want to delete any Data Bite, so that outdated or wrong content can be fully removed; Analysts cannot delete Data Bites.\n14. As an Analyst, I want to create a **draft** Report (title, slug, Markdown body) and optionally attach a file (e.g. the Factbook PDF, when an official document exists), so that I can prepare the next cited document.\n15. As an Analyst, I want to edit a draft Report — its Markdown body, or the attached file — including one another Analyst started, so that shift changes don't block prep work.\n16. As an Analyst, I want to be unable to publish, edit, unpublish, or delete a Report once it's published, so that cited documents can't be changed or taken down without Director review.\n17. As a Director, I want to publish, edit (Markdown body or the attached file), or unpublish any Report, so that I control what's cited externally.\n18. As a Director, I want to delete any Report, so that outdated cited documents can be fully removed.\n19. As a Director or Analyst, I want a dashboard showing counts of Data Bites and Reports by status, so that I can see the state of the office's content at a glance.\n20. As a Director or Analyst, I want to filter the content list by type (Data Bite/Report) and status (draft/published), so that I can find something quickly.\n21. As a Director or Analyst, I want a Markdown preview while editing a Data Bite or a Report's caption, so that I can see how it will actually render (post-sanitization) before publishing.\n22. As a visitor, I want to see only published Data Bites and Reports on the public site, so that I never see draft or unreleased data, and never a draft's uploaded file.\n23. As a visitor, I want published Reports to appear in the public site's navigation, so that I can find the Factbook or Common Data Set without searching.\n24. As a visitor, I want to read a published Report's Markdown body on its page, and download its attached file if one exists, so that I get the full content plus the official document when available.\n25. As a Director, I want to run `cms publish` and have it regenerate `site/` from only published content, writing Data Bites to `data-bites/<slug>.html`, Reports to `reports/<slug>.html`, and Report files to `reports/files/<slug>.<ext>`, all with relative paths, so that the export works correctly on GitHub Pages and nothing collides.\n26. As an Analyst or Director, I want a duplicate slug within the same content type rejected at save time with a clear error, so that two items can never overwrite each other's export path.\n27. As an uploader, I want a Report's optional attached file restricted to an allowlisted file type (PDF) and a size cap, so that the upload path can't be used to smuggle arbitrary or oversized files.\n28. As anyone, I want content, files, and sessions to survive a server restart, so that nothing is lost between work sessions.\n29. As a security-conscious Director, I want passwords stored as argon2 hashes and every state-changing form — including login and logout — protected by a CSRF token, so that the office's accounts and content can't be trivially compromised.\n30. As a security-conscious Director, I want Markdown (Data Bite bodies, Report captions) stored raw and sanitized only at render time (preview and export), so that a pasted `<script>` tag can never execute anywhere it's displayed.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n- **Roles**: internal role values stay `admin` and `editor` (compatibility with `tests/conftest.py`'s `DEMO_USERS` and `client_as` fixture); templates display them as \"Director\" and \"Analyst\" respectively. No third role.\n- **DataBite**: title, slug, raw Markdown body, status (`draft`/`published`), author reference, `created_at`/`updated_at`.\n- **Report**: title, slug, required raw Markdown body (same shape as `DataBite`, satisfies core rubric C4), optional attached file (PDF; allowlisted extension + MIME type; size-capped — exact cap is an implementation detail, pick something generous but bounded, e.g. 20MB), status, author reference, `created_at`/`updated_at`. The optional-file part doubles as the \"Media uploads\" stretch goal.\n- **Shift-continuity editing**: any Analyst may edit an item (Data Bite, or a Report while still draft) regardless of who created or last edited it; the original author and `created_at` are preserved, only `updated_at` changes.\n- **Permission rule** (ADR-003): Analysts — full create/edit/publish/unpublish on `DataBite`, no delete. Analysts — create/edit on **draft** `Report` only (Markdown body and/or the optional attached file); once a `Report` is published, only the Director may edit, unpublish, or delete it, and only the Director may ever publish one. Directors — full CRUD + publish on both, plus user management. Enforced server-side on every route — hiding a link is not access control (Security Checklist item 10).\n- **Auth**: every `/admin/*` route requires an authenticated session (redirect to login if anonymous). Every protected request re-validates the session's user as active server-side — a deactivated user's existing signed cookie must fail on the next request, not just on next login. Session via signed cookies (`itsdangerous`), password hashing via `argon2-cffi`.\n- **CSRF**: a per-session token embedded in every state-changing form and validated server-side on POST, including `/login` and `/logout`.\n- **Markdown/sanitization pipeline**: raw Markdown is what's stored (Data Bite bodies, Report captions). Rendering (preview or export) always runs `markdown-it-py` → `nh3` before display; pre-rendered/sanitized HTML is never persisted.\n- **File uploads (optional)**: handled via the existing `python-multipart` dependency (no new dependency needed). When present, the file is stored outside `site/` (e.g. a local `uploads/` directory, gitignored like the DB) until publish; a draft Report's file must never appear in `site/`, same rule as draft content generally. A Report with no attached file exports as a plain Markdown page, same as a Data Bite.\n- **Publish gate & export paths**: an item reaches `site/` only when `status == published`. `cms publish` (`app/publish.py`) writes published `DataBite` rows to `data-bites/<slug>.html`, published `Report` rows to `reports/<slug>.html` (rendering the Markdown body, plus a download link when a file is attached), and copies each published Report's attached file, if any, to `reports/files/<slug>.<ext>` — all relative paths. Namespacing by type means a Data Bite and a Report may share a slug; two items of the *same* type sharing a slug is rejected at save time with a form validation error.\n- **Navigation**: public nav is built from published `Report`s only.\n- **Individual Analyst accounts** (ADR-003, reversed from an earlier shared-account draft): each intern gets their own named Analyst-role account, created and deactivated by the Director as rotations change. Shift continuity (any Analyst can edit any other Analyst's draft) is preserved as a permission rule, not by sharing a login — author and created_at are unaffected by who edits.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- All tests exercise real HTTP behavior through `TestClient` via the existing `client` / `client_as(role)` fixtures in `tests/conftest.py` — no isolated unit-testing of permission logic. `DEMO_USERS`/`client_as` keeps using `\"admin\"`/`\"editor\"` role keys (per the internal-role-values decision above); UI-label tests separately assert \"Director\"/\"Analyst\" appear in rendered admin templates.\n- Prior art: `tests/test_t00.py` — status-code and text-content assertions against real requests, extended with DB-state and exported-file assertions where relevant.\n- Access-control tests per route: every Director-only route rejects an Analyst session (403/redirect) and rejects an anonymous session (redirect to login) — Security Checklist items 4–5, rubric D3. Includes a specific test that a deactivated user's still-valid-looking session cookie is rejected on the next request.\n- CSRF tests: replay one POST per state-changing form (including login and logout) with the token stripped, expect rejection — Security Checklist item 3, rubric D2.\n- Sanitization test: save a `<script>`/`onerror=` payload in a Data Bite body and a Report body, publish, assert it's inert in both the admin preview and the exported HTML — Security Checklist item 7, rubric D4.\n- Draft-leak test: publish with a draft Data Bite and a draft Report (with its file) present, assert both are absent from `site/` — Security Checklist item 8.\n- Upload-validation test: a non-PDF or oversized attached file is rejected at upload time with a clear error; a Report with no attached file still publishes and exports correctly.\n- Slug-collision test: saving a second item of the same content type with an existing slug is rejected with a validation error, not a silent overwrite.\n- Published-Report lockdown test: an Analyst attempting to edit (Markdown body or attached file), unpublish, or delete an already-published Report is rejected, even though they could when it was a draft.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\nPer the manual and rubric: multi-site support, drag-and-drop page builders, rich-text WYSIWYG editors. Also out of scope for the core spec (available separately as `stretch` tickets if pursued): categories/tags, revision history/rollback, scheduled publishing, site search, comments/moderation, a plugin/hook system, switchable themes, per-intern individual accounts (deliberately rejected, see ADR-003), uploads for Data Bites (Data Bites stay Markdown-only; only Reports may optionally carry an attached file).\n\n## Further Notes\n\nThis spec covers all 7 required capabilities from the rubric, plus the \"Media uploads\" stretch goal folded into the Report content type. `/to-tickets` will slice this into 7–9 vertical slices, each touching data model + route + template + test per rubric B3.\n\n\n----T03\n## Parent\n\nPart of #1\n\n## What to build\n\nAnalyst can create, edit, publish, and unpublish a Data Bite; Director can additionally delete one. Drafts never leak into any public-facing query. Slug uniqueness is enforced within Data Bites. Any Analyst can edit any Data Bite (shift continuity) while the original author and created_at are preserved.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] Analyst can create a Data Bite (title, slug, Markdown body) as a draft\n- [ ] Analyst can publish and unpublish a Data Bite\n- [ ] Analyst cannot delete a Data Bite (403/redirect); Director can\n- [ ] A second Data Bite with a duplicate slug is rejected with a validation error\n- [ ] Editing a Data Bite preserves the original author and created_at; only updated_at changes\n- [ ] Any Analyst (not just the original author) can edit any Data Bite\n- [ ] A draft Data Bite is never returned by any public-facing query\n- [ ] All state-changing POSTs require CSRF; anonymous/Analyst access is checked where restricted\n- [ ] Tests cover all of the above\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- #2 (T01)\n## /code-review findings for T03 and how each was resolved\n\nTwo-axis review (Standards and Spec) of the T03 diff against `b9fcf90`.\n\n### Standards: no hard violations\nCSRF is on every new POST. Delete is Director-only in code (`require_director`), not just a hidden button. The public list is relative-path only and published-only. No new dependencies.\n\n| Finding | Resolution |\n|---|---|\n| Module docstring mentions \"`*_published` functions\" but only `list_published` exists | **Fixed**: the docstring now names `list_published` |\n| Possible Speculative Generality: `set_status` re-validates status that is only ever passed as a literal (the DB `CHECK` enforces it anyway) | **Fixed**: check removed |\n| Possible Duplicated Code: `edit_bite` checked for 404 twice (`_get_or_404` then `if not found`) | **Fixed**: the redundant second check is removed |\n| Possible Mysterious Name: `_clean`, `_duplicate` | **Fixed**: renamed to `_validate` and `_duplicate_slug_error` |\n| Possible Feature Envy: `main.py` built the `data-bites/<slug>.html` href | **Fixed**: the href was removed (see the Spec finding below) |\n| Possible Data Clumps (`title, slug, body`) and page-helper shape repeated from `_users_page` | **Kept**: matches the T02 convention. Revisit if T04 Reports repeat it a third time |\n| Possible Primitive Obsession: `\"draft\"`/`\"published\"` strings | **Kept**: two values, enforced by a DB `CHECK` |\n| \"Unpublish\" vs CONTEXT.md's *Avoid: Unpublished* | **Kept**: it is the verb the spec uses. The resulting status is labelled **Draft** |\n\n### Spec: all acceptance criteria met\n\n| Finding | Resolution |\n|---|---|\n| Scope creep: the public `/` linked to `data-bites/<slug>.html`, which 404s until T07 | **Fixed**: `/` lists published Data Bite titles without links, and the home template links only when an item has an `href`. T07 adds the pages and links |\n| Every `sqlite3.IntegrityError` was reported as a duplicate slug | **Fixed**: only a `data_bites.slug` UNIQUE violation becomes the validation error; anything else re-raises |\n| Publish and unpublish bump `updated_at` | **Kept, deliberate**: a status change counts as a change. Author and `created_at` are never written after insert |\n| Draft-leak test covers the live `/` only | **Accepted**: the `site/` draft-leak test belongs to T08 |\n| Spec #1's *Out of Scope* still lists \"per-intern individual accounts (deliberately rejected)\", which contradicts ADR-003 | **Not changed here**: the spec issue needs a one-line edit, flagged to the owner |\n\nTests: `tests/test_t03_data_bites.py` has 22 tests, and T00–T03 total 71 passed. Two tests passed on their first run, so I mutation-checked them to confirm they can fail: the duplicate-slug test (UNIQUE constraint removed) and the CSRF test (`require_csrf` dropped from publish). Both failed as expected.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"d8b1dfcb-01ba-476e-bf8e-e4c3e30f2015","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:09:47.331Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"T04: Reports (draft/publish lockdown)\n## Parent\n\nPart of #1\n\n## What to build\n\nAnalyst can create and edit a draft Report (title, slug, required Markdown body). Once published, only Director can edit, unpublish, delete, or publish it. Slug uniqueness is enforced within Reports, independently of Data Bites (the same slug may be reused across the two content types, per ADR-003's export-path namespacing).\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] Analyst can create a draft Report with a required Markdown body\n- [ ] Analyst can edit a draft Report, including one another Analyst started\n- [ ] Analyst gets 403/redirect attempting to publish a Report\n- [ ] Once published, Analyst gets 403/redirect attempting to edit, unpublish, or delete it\n- [ ] Director can publish, edit, unpublish, and delete a Report in any state\n- [ ] A second Report with a duplicate slug is rejected with a validation error; a Data Bite may share a slug with a Report without conflict\n- [ ] A draft Report is never returned by any public-facing query\n- [ ] All state-changing POSTs require CSRF\n- [ ] Tests cover all of the above\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- #2 (T01)\n-----SPEC\n## Problem Statement\n\nKenyon's Office of Institutional Research (Director + rotating student Analysts) currently publishes Data Bites and Reports by emailing files around and dropping them in a shared OneDrive folder. With multiple interns working at once, nobody can tell which version is current, and people outside the office resort to emailing IR directly to ask for the latest Factbook instead of finding it themselves.\n\n## Solution\n\nA CMS with two roles — Director (admin) and Analyst (editor; each intern gets their own named account) — that separates draft from published content. Analysts can draft, edit, publish, and unpublish Data Bites (short, dated Markdown items) without waiting on the Director. Reports (Factbook, Common Data Set, Survey Policy) are Markdown pages, same as Data Bites, with an optional attached file (e.g. the actual Factbook PDF, when one exists) — matching how these documents actually circulate while keeping the core rubric's required Markdown-body field intact. Only the Director can publish, edit, unpublish, or delete a Report once it exists. `cms publish` exports only published content to a static public site on GitHub Pages, with content-type-namespaced paths.\n\n## User Stories\n\n1. As a Director, I want to log in with a password, so that I can access the admin console.\n2. As an Analyst, I want to log in with my own account, so that whichever intern is on shift can post routine content under their own name.\n3. As anyone, I want a wrong password rejected with a generic message, so that the login form doesn't reveal whether an account exists.\n4. As any authenticated user (Director or Analyst), I want to log out, so that a shared machine doesn't stay signed in.\n5. As a Director, I want to create a new user account (Director or Analyst), so that I can grant access as staffing changes.\n6. As a Director, I want to change a user's role, so that I can correct access if someone's responsibilities change.\n7. As a Director, I want to deactivate a user, so that someone who's left the office can't log in anymore — even with a browser tab still holding an old session cookie — without deleting their history.\n8. As an Analyst, I want the admin console to hide user management entirely, so that I'm not tempted or able to touch accounts.\n9. As an Analyst, I want to create a Data Bite (title, slug, Markdown body), so that I can post a survey result or announcement.\n10. As an Analyst, I want to save a Data Bite as a draft, so that I can work on it across multiple sessions before it's ready.\n11. As an Analyst, I want to publish a Data Bite myself, so that I don't have to wait on the Director for routine content.\n12. As an Analyst, I want to edit or unpublish a Data Bite (including one another Analyst drafted or published), so that shift changes don't block fixing a mistake.\n13. As a Director, I want to delete any Data Bite, so that outdated or wrong content can be fully removed; Analysts cannot delete Data Bites.\n14. As an Analyst, I want to create a **draft** Report (title, slug, Markdown body) and optionally attach a file (e.g. the Factbook PDF, when an official document exists), so that I can prepare the next cited document.\n15. As an Analyst, I want to edit a draft Report — its Markdown body, or the attached file — including one another Analyst started, so that shift changes don't block prep work.\n16. As an Analyst, I want to be unable to publish, edit, unpublish, or delete a Report once it's published, so that cited documents can't be changed or taken down without Director review.\n17. As a Director, I want to publish, edit (Markdown body or the attached file), or unpublish any Report, so that I control what's cited externally.\n18. As a Director, I want to delete any Report, so that outdated cited documents can be fully removed.\n19. As a Director or Analyst, I want a dashboard showing counts of Data Bites and Reports by status, so that I can see the state of the office's content at a glance.\n20. As a Director or Analyst, I want to filter the content list by type (Data Bite/Report) and status (draft/published), so that I can find something quickly.\n21. As a Director or Analyst, I want a Markdown preview while editing a Data Bite or a Report's caption, so that I can see how it will actually render (post-sanitization) before publishing.\n22. As a visitor, I want to see only published Data Bites and Reports on the public site, so that I never see draft or unreleased data, and never a draft's uploaded file.\n23. As a visitor, I want published Reports to appear in the public site's navigation, so that I can find the Factbook or Common Data Set without searching.\n24. As a visitor, I want to read a published Report's Markdown body on its page, and download its attached file if one exists, so that I get the full content plus the official document when available.\n25. As a Director, I want to run `cms publish` and have it regenerate `site/` from only published content, writing Data Bites to `data-bites/<slug>.html`, Reports to `reports/<slug>.html`, and Report files to `reports/files/<slug>.<ext>`, all with relative paths, so that the export works correctly on GitHub Pages and nothing collides.\n26. As an Analyst or Director, I want a duplicate slug within the same content type rejected at save time with a clear error, so that two items can never overwrite each other's export path.\n27. As an uploader, I want a Report's optional attached file restricted to an allowlisted file type (PDF) and a size cap, so that the upload path can't be used to smuggle arbitrary or oversized files.\n28. As anyone, I want content, files, and sessions to survive a server restart, so that nothing is lost between work sessions.\n29. As a security-conscious Director, I want passwords stored as argon2 hashes and every state-changing form — including login and logout — protected by a CSRF token, so that the office's accounts and content can't be trivially compromised.\n30. As a security-conscious Director, I want Markdown (Data Bite bodies, Report captions) stored raw and sanitized only at render time (preview and export), so that a pasted `<script>` tag can never execute anywhere it's displayed.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n- **Roles**: internal role values stay `admin` and `editor` (compatibility with `tests/conftest.py`'s `DEMO_USERS` and `client_as` fixture); templates display them as \"Director\" and \"Analyst\" respectively. No third role.\n- **DataBite**: title, slug, raw Markdown body, status (`draft`/`published`), author reference, `created_at`/`updated_at`.\n- **Report**: title, slug, required raw Markdown body (same shape as `DataBite`, satisfies core rubric C4), optional attached file (PDF; allowlisted extension + MIME type; size-capped — exact cap is an implementation detail, pick something generous but bounded, e.g. 20MB), status, author reference, `created_at`/`updated_at`. The optional-file part doubles as the \"Media uploads\" stretch goal.\n- **Shift-continuity editing**: any Analyst may edit an item (Data Bite, or a Report while still draft) regardless of who created or last edited it; the original author and `created_at` are preserved, only `updated_at` changes.\n- **Permission rule** (ADR-003): Analysts — full create/edit/publish/unpublish on `DataBite`, no delete. Analysts — create/edit on **draft** `Report` only (Markdown body and/or the optional attached file); once a `Report` is published, only the Director may edit, unpublish, or delete it, and only the Director may ever publish one. Directors — full CRUD + publish on both, plus user management. Enforced server-side on every route — hiding a link is not access control (Security Checklist item 10).\n- **Auth**: every `/admin/*` route requires an authenticated session (redirect to login if anonymous). Every protected request re-validates the session's user as active server-side — a deactivated user's existing signed cookie must fail on the next request, not just on next login. Session via signed cookies (`itsdangerous`), password hashing via `argon2-cffi`.\n- **CSRF**: a per-session token embedded in every state-changing form and validated server-side on POST, including `/login` and `/logout`.\n- **Markdown/sanitization pipeline**: raw Markdown is what's stored (Data Bite bodies, Report captions). Rendering (preview or export) always runs `markdown-it-py` → `nh3` before display; pre-rendered/sanitized HTML is never persisted.\n- **File uploads (optional)**: handled via the existing `python-multipart` dependency (no new dependency needed). When present, the file is stored outside `site/` (e.g. a local `uploads/` directory, gitignored like the DB) until publish; a draft Report's file must never appear in `site/`, same rule as draft content generally. A Report with no attached file exports as a plain Markdown page, same as a Data Bite.\n- **Publish gate & export paths**: an item reaches `site/` only when `status == published`. `cms publish` (`app/publish.py`) writes published `DataBite` rows to `data-bites/<slug>.html`, published `Report` rows to `reports/<slug>.html` (rendering the Markdown body, plus a download link when a file is attached), and copies each published Report's attached file, if any, to `reports/files/<slug>.<ext>` — all relative paths. Namespacing by type means a Data Bite and a Report may share a slug; two items of the *same* type sharing a slug is rejected at save time with a form validation error.\n- **Navigation**: public nav is built from published `Report`s only.\n- **Individual Analyst accounts** (ADR-003, reversed from an earlier shared-account draft): each intern gets their own named Analyst-role account, created and deactivated by the Director as rotations change. Shift continuity (any Analyst can edit any other Analyst's draft) is preserved as a permission rule, not by sharing a login — author and created_at are unaffected by who edits.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- All tests exercise real HTTP behavior through `TestClient` via the existing `client` / `client_as(role)` fixtures in `tests/conftest.py` — no isolated unit-testing of permission logic. `DEMO_USERS`/`client_as` keeps using `\"admin\"`/`\"editor\"` role keys (per the internal-role-values decision above); UI-label tests separately assert \"Director\"/\"Analyst\" appear in rendered admin templates.\n- Prior art: `tests/test_t00.py` — status-code and text-content assertions against real requests, extended with DB-state and exported-file assertions where relevant.\n- Access-control tests per route: every Director-only route rejects an Analyst session (403/redirect) and rejects an anonymous session (redirect to login) — Security Checklist items 4–5, rubric D3. Includes a specific test that a deactivated user's still-valid-looking session cookie is rejected on the next request.\n- CSRF tests: replay one POST per state-changing form (including login and logout) with the token stripped, expect rejection — Security Checklist item 3, rubric D2.\n- Sanitization test: save a `<script>`/`onerror=` payload in a Data Bite body and a Report body, publish, assert it's inert in both the admin preview and the exported HTML — Security Checklist item 7, rubric D4.\n- Draft-leak test: publish with a draft Data Bite and a draft Report (with its file) present, assert both are absent from `site/` — Security Checklist item 8.\n- Upload-validation test: a non-PDF or oversized attached file is rejected at upload time with a clear error; a Report with no attached file still publishes and exports correctly.\n- Slug-collision test: saving a second item of the same content type with an existing slug is rejected with a validation error, not a silent overwrite.\n- Published-Report lockdown test: an Analyst attempting to edit (Markdown body or attached file), unpublish, or delete an already-published Report is rejected, even though they could when it was a draft.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\nPer the manual and rubric: multi-site support, drag-and-drop page builders, rich-text WYSIWYG editors. Also out of scope for the core spec (available separately as `stretch` tickets if pursued): categories/tags, revision history/rollback, scheduled publishing, site search, comments/moderation, a plugin/hook system, switchable themes, per-intern individual accounts (deliberately rejected, see ADR-003), uploads for Data Bites (Data Bites stay Markdown-only; only Reports may optionally carry an attached file).\n\n## Further Notes\n\nThis spec covers all 7 required capabilities from the rubric, plus the \"Media uploads\" stretch goal folded into the Report content type. `/to-tickets` will slice this into 7–9 vertical slices, each touching data model + route + template + test per rubric B3.\n\n\n----T03\n## Parent\n\nPart of #1\n\n## What to build\n\nAnalyst can create, edit, publish, and unpublish a Data Bite; Director can additionally delete one. Drafts never leak into any public-facing query. Slug uniqueness is enforced within Data Bites. Any Analyst can edit any Data Bite (shift continuity) while the original author and created_at are preserved.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] Analyst can create a Data Bite (title, slug, Markdown body) as a draft\n- [ ] Analyst can publish and unpublish a Data Bite\n- [ ] Analyst cannot delete a Data Bite (403/redirect); Director can\n- [ ] A second Data Bite with a duplicate slug is rejected with a validation error\n- [ ] Editing a Data Bite preserves the original author and created_at; only updated_at changes\n- [ ] Any Analyst (not just the original author) can edit any Data Bite\n- [ ] A draft Data Bite is never returned by any public-facing query\n- [ ] All state-changing POSTs require CSRF; anonymous/Analyst access is checked where restricted\n- [ ] Tests cover all of the above\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- #2 (T01)\n## /code-review findings for T03 and how each was resolved\n\nTwo-axis review (Standards and Spec) of the T03 diff against `b9fcf90`.\n\n### Standards: no hard violations\nCSRF is on every new POST. Delete is Director-only in code (`require_director`), not just a hidden button. The public list is relative-path only and published-only. No new dependencies.\n\n| Finding | Resolution |\n|---|---|\n| Module docstring mentions \"`*_published` functions\" but only `list_published` exists | **Fixed**: the docstring now names `list_published` |\n| Possible Speculative Generality: `set_status` re-validates status that is only ever passed as a literal (the DB `CHECK` enforces it anyway) | **Fixed**: check removed |\n| Possible Duplicated Code: `edit_bite` checked for 404 twice (`_get_or_404` then `if not found`) | **Fixed**: the redundant second check is removed |\n| Possible Mysterious Name: `_clean`, `_duplicate` | **Fixed**: renamed to `_validate` and `_duplicate_slug_error` |\n| Possible Feature Envy: `main.py` built the `data-bites/<slug>.html` href | **Fixed**: the href was removed (see the Spec finding below) |\n| Possible Data Clumps (`title, slug, body`) and page-helper shape repeated from `_users_page` | **Kept**: matches the T02 convention. Revisit if T04 Reports repeat it a third time |\n| Possible Primitive Obsession: `\"draft\"`/`\"published\"` strings | **Kept**: two values, enforced by a DB `CHECK` |\n| \"Unpublish\" vs CONTEXT.md's *Avoid: Unpublished* | **Kept**: it is the verb the spec uses. The resulting status is labelled **Draft** |\n\n### Spec: all acceptance criteria met\n\n| Finding | Resolution |\n|---|---|\n| Scope creep: the public `/` linked to `data-bites/<slug>.html`, which 404s until T07 | **Fixed**: `/` lists published Data Bite titles without links, and the home template links only when an item has an `href`. T07 adds the pages and links |\n| Every `sqlite3.IntegrityError` was reported as a duplicate slug | **Fixed**: only a `data_bites.slug` UNIQUE violation becomes the validation error; anything else re-raises |\n| Publish and unpublish bump `updated_at` | **Kept, deliberate**: a status change counts as a change. Author and `created_at` are never written after insert |\n| Draft-leak test covers the live `/` only | **Accepted**: the `site/` draft-leak test belongs to T08 |\n| Spec #1's *Out of Scope* still lists \"per-intern individual accounts (deliberately rejected)\", which contradicts ADR-003 | **Not changed here**: the spec issue needs a one-line edit, flagged to the owner |\n\nTests: `tests/test_t03_data_bites.py` has 22 tests, and T00–T03 total 71 passed. Two tests passed on their first run, so I mutation-checked them to confirm they can fail: the duplicate-slug test (UNIQUE constraint removed) and the CSRF test (`require_csrf` dropped from publish). Both failed as expected.","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"edcc246d-fecf-4edb-94b4-7bdab820f6f4","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"4279e215-2984-45fe-8b76-5000acd509a0","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"04e24caf-e76d-4ac6-b3f7-74cb97b3f994","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0141rbzbGosdWnrYYBdgafiJ","type":"tool_result","content":"commit 4b4d114ab4389e9fe401550d6b93ca1be7be236b\nAuthor: Davelle Ampofo-Twumasi <[REDACTED-EMAIL]>\nDate:   Mon Sep 28 18:08:26 2026 -0400\n\n    T03: Data Bites — create, edit, publish, unpublish, delete\n    \n    - data_bites table (title, unique slug, raw Markdown body, draft/published\n      status, author, created_at/updated_at) and app/data_bites.py.\n    - /admin/data-bites: any signed-in user lists, creates (always as a draft),\n      edits, publishes and unpublishes any Data Bite (shift continuity,\n      ADR-003). Editing keeps the author and created_at; updated_at changes.\n      Delete is Director only, enforced by require_director (Analyst gets 403)\n      and not offered in an Analyst's UI. {"parentUuid":"00e7ae21-6a97-4596-805a-bd708fbc190a","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"04e24caf-e76d-4ac6-b3f7-74cb97b3f994","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Gbp7RMngXqMjWF7eKhQHcq","type":"tool_result","content":"##### app/data_bites.py\n     1\t\"\"\"Data Bites: short, dated Markdown items.\n     2\t\n     3\tAny Analyst or Director may create, edit, publish, and unpublish any Data Bite\n     4\t(shift continuity, ADR-003); only a Director may delete one, which the routes\n     5\tenforce. Editing never changes the author or created_at.\n     6\t\n     7\tPublic-facing code reads Data Bites only through `list_published`, so a draft\n     8\tcannot leak through it.\n     9\t\"\"\"\n    10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n    11\t\n    12\timport re\n    13\timport sqlite3\n    14\t\n    15\tfrom app import db\n    16\t\n    17\tSTATUS_LABELS = {\"draft\": \"Draft\", \"published\": \"Published\"}\n    18\t_SLUG_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*\")\n    19\t_SELECT = (\"SELECT d.*, u.name AS author_name FROM data_bites d\"\n    20\t           \" JOIN users u ON u.id = d.author_id\")\n    21\t\n    22\t\n    23\tclass DataBiteError(ValueError):\n    24\t    \"\"\"A Data Bite broke a rule; the message is safe to show.\"\"\"\n    25\t\n    26\t\n    27\tclass DuplicateSlug(DataBiteError):\n    28\t    pass\n    29\t\n    30\t\n    31\tdef _validate(title: str, slug: str, body: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:\n    32\t    title, slug = title.strip(), slug.strip()\n    33\t    if not title:\n    34\t        raise DataBiteError(\"Enter a title.\")\n    35\t    if not _SLUG_PATTERN.fullmatch(slug):\n    36\t        raise DataBiteError(\"Slugs use lowercase letters, digits, and single \"\n    37\t                            \"hyphens, e.g. fall-enrollment.\")\n    38\t    if not body.strip():\n    39\t        raise DataBiteError(\"Enter a body.\")\n    40\t    return title, slug, body\n    41\t\n    42\t\n    43\tdef _duplicate_slug_error(exc: sqlite3.IntegrityError, slug: str) -> DataBiteError:\n    44\t    \"\"\"Translate the slug's UNIQUE violation; re-raise any other constraint.\"\"\"\n    45\t    if \"data_bites.slug\" not in str(exc):\n    46\t        raise exc\n    47\t    return DuplicateSlug(f\"Another Data Bite already uses the slug {slug}.\")\n    48\t\n    49\t\n    50\tdef create(title: str, slug: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    51\t    \"\"\"Create a draft Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    52\t    title, slug, body = _validate(title, slug, body)\n    53\t    try:\n    54\t        with db.connect() as conn:\n    55\t            cursor = conn.execute(\n    56\t                \"INSERT INTO data_bites (title, slug, body, author_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n    57\t                (title, slug, body, author_id),\n    58\t            )\n    59\t    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n    60\t        raise _duplicate_slug_error(exc, slug) from None\n    61\t    return cursor.lastrowid\n    62\t\n    63\t\n    64\tdef update(bite_id: int, title: str, slug: str, body: str) -> bool:\n    65\t    \"\"\"Edit title, slug, and body. The author, created_at, and status are kept.\n    66\t    False if there is no such Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    67\t    title, slug, body = _validate(title, slug, body)\n    68\t    try:\n    69\t        with db.connect() as conn:\n    70\t            return conn.execute(\n    71\t                \"UPDATE data_bites SET title = ?, slug = ?, body = ?,\"\n    72\t                \" updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?\",\n    73\t                (title, slug, body, bite_id),\n    74\t            ).rowcount == 1\n    75\t    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n    76\t        raise _duplicate_slug_error(exc, slug) from None\n    77\t\n    78\t\n    79\tdef get(bite_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    80\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n    81\t        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} WHERE d.id = ?\", (bite_id,)).fetchone()\n    82\t\n    83\t\n    84\tdef list_all() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    85\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n    86\t        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} ORDER BY d.updated_at DESC, d.id DESC\").fetchall()\n    87\t\n    88\t\n    89\tdef set_status(bite_id: int, status: str) -> bool:\n    90\t    \"\"\"Publish or unpublish. False if there is no such Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    91\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n    92\t        return conn.execute(\n    93\t            \"UPDATE data_bites SET status = ?, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?\",\n    94\t            (status, bite_id),\n    95\t        ).rowcount == 1\n    96\t\n    97\t\n    98\tdef delete(bite_id: int) -> bool:\n    99\t    \"\"\"False if there is no such Data Bite.\"\"\"\n   100\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n   101\t        return conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM data_bites WHERE id = ?\", (bite_id,)).rowcount == 1\n   102\t\n   103\t\n   104\tdef list_published() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n   105\t    \"\"\"The public-facing query: published Data Bites only, newest first.\"\"\"\n   106\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n   107\t        return conn.execute(\n   108\t            f\"{_SELECT} WHERE d.status = 'published' ORDER BY d.created_at DESC, d.id DESC\"\n   109\t        ).fetchall()\n##### app/db.py\n     1\t\"\"\"SQLite access. Every table's schema lives here, so init_db() builds them all.\"\"\"\n     2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     3\t\n     4\timport sqlite3\n     5\tfrom collections.abc import Iterator\n     6\tfrom contextlib import contextmanager\n     7\t\n     8\tfrom app import settings\n     9\t\n    10\tSCHEMA = \"\"\"\n    11\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    12\t    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    13\t    email         TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    14\t    name          TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    15\t    role          TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    16\t    password_hash TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    17\t    is_active     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    18\t    created_at    TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n    19\t);\n    20\t\n    21\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS data_bites (\n    22\t    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    23\t    title      TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    24\t    slug       TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    25\t    body       TEXT    NOT NULL,  -- raw Markdown; sanitized only when rendered\n    26\t    status     TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    27\t    author_id  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    28\t    created_at TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),\n    29\t    updated_at TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n    30\t);\n    31\t\"\"\"\n    32\t\n    33\t\n    34\t@contextmanager\n    35\tdef connect() -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    36\t    \"\"\"Open the database named by settings (read at call time, so tests can swap\n    37\t    it). Commits on success, rolls back on error, always closes.\"\"\"\n    38\t    conn = sqlite3.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    39\t    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    40\t    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n    41\t    try:\n    42\t        with conn:\n    43\t            yield conn\n    44\t    finally:\n    45\t        conn.close()\n    46\t\n    47\t\n    48\tdef init_db() -> None:\n    49\t    with connect() as conn:\n    50\t        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n##### app/main.py\n     1\t\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n     2\t\n     3\tThe admin console lives under /admin (login required) and the public preview\n     4\tat /. Routes live in their own modules under app/routes/ and are included here.\n     5\tKeep this file small.\n     6\t\"\"\"\n     7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     8\t\n     9\timport sys\n    10\tfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\n    11\t\n    12\tfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, HTTPException, Request\n    13\tfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n    14\tfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n    15\t\n    16\tfrom app import db, settings\n    17\tfrom app.auth import LoginRequired, current_user\n    18\tfrom app.data_bites import list_published as published_data_bites\n    19\tfrom app.routes import admin, auth, data_bites, users\n    20\tfrom app.templating import templates\n    21\t\n    22\tSESSION_MAX_AGE = 8 * 60 * 60  # one working day\n    23\tALL_METHODS = [\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"PATCH\", \"DELETE\", \"OPTIONS\"]\n    24\t\n    25\t\n    26\t@asynccontextmanager\n    27\tasync def lifespan(app: FastAPI):\n    28\t    # At server start, not import, so importing app.main never touches a database.\n    29\t    db.init_db()\n    30\t    if settings.SECRET_KEY_IS_PLACEHOLDER:\n    31\t        print(\"WARNING: CMS_SECRET_KEY is unset or the .env.example placeholder; \"\n    32\t              \"session cookies can be forged. Set a long random value in .env.\",\n    33\t              file=sys.stderr)\n    34\t    yield\n    35\t\n    36\t\n    37\tdef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    38\t    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\", lifespan=lifespan)\n    39\t    app.add_middleware(\n    40\t        SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n    41\t        session_cookie=\"cms_session\", max_age=SESSION_MAX_AGE, same_site=\"lax\",\n    42\t    )\n    43\t\n    44\t    @app.exception_handler(LoginRequired)\n    45\t    def redirect_to_login(request: Request, exc: LoginRequired):\n    46\t        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    47\t\n    48\t    @app.get(\"/\")\n    49\t    def public_home(request: Request):\n    50\t        # Titles only: the public Data Bite pages (and links to them) arrive\n    51\t        # with the public-site ticket, T07.\n    52\t        items = [{\"title\": b[\"title\"]} for b in published_data_bites()]\n    53\t        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n    54\t            request, \"public/home.html\",\n    55\t            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": items},\n    56\t        )\n    57\t\n    58\t    app.include_router(auth.router)\n    59\t    app.include_router(admin.router)\n    60\t    app.include_router(users.router)\n    61\t    app.include_router(data_bites.router)\n    62\t\n    63\t    # Must stay last: any /admin request no router above claimed, by path or by\n    64\t    # method. Anonymous visitors are sent to login (so they can't probe which\n    65\t    # admin paths exist); signed-in users get 405/404.\n    66\t    @app.api_route(\"/admin\", methods=ALL_METHODS,\n    67\t                   dependencies=[Depends(current_user)], include_in_schema=False)\n    68\t    def admin_method_not_allowed():\n    69\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=405)\n    70\t\n    71\t    @app.api_route(\"/admin/{rest:path}\", methods=ALL_METHODS,\n    72\t                   dependencies=[Depends(current_user)], include_in_schema=False)\n    73\t    def admin_not_found(rest: str):\n    74\t        if rest == \"\":\n    75\t            return RedirectResponse(\"/admin\", status_code=307)\n    76\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n    77\t\n    78\t    return app\n    79\t\n    80\t\n    81\tapp = create_app()\n##### app/routes/data_bites.py\n     1\t\"\"\"Data Bites in the admin console. Any signed-in user may do everything here\n     2\texcept delete, which is Director only.\"\"\"\n     3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     4\t\n     5\timport sqlite3\n     6\t\n     7\tfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\n     8\tfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n     9\t\n    10\tfrom app import data_bites\n    11\tfrom app.auth import current_user, require_csrf, require_director\n    12\tfrom app.templating import templates\n    13\t\n    14\trouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/data-bites\", dependencies=[Depends(current_user)])\n    15\t\n    16\t\n    17\tdef _list_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, *, error: str | None = None,\n    18\t               form: dict | None = None, status_code: int = 200):\n    19\t    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n    20\t        request, \"admin/data_bites.html\",\n    21\t        {\"title\": \"Data Bites\", \"home_path\": \"/admin\", \"user\": user,\n    22\t         \"bites\": data_bites.list_all(), \"status_labels\": data_bites.STATUS_LABELS,\n    23\t         \"error\": error, \"form\": form or {}},\n    24\t        status_code=status_code,\n    25\t    )\n    26\t\n    27\t\n    28\t@router.get(\"\")\n    29\tdef list_bites(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    30\t    return _list_page(request, user)\n    31\t\n    32\t\n    33\t@router.post(\"\", dependencies=[Depends(require_csrf)])\n    34\tdef create_bite(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n    35\t                slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\")):\n    36\t    try:\n    37\t        data_bites.create(title, slug, body, user[\"id\"])\n    38\t    except data_bites.DataBiteError as exc:\n    39\t        return _list_page(request, user, error=str(exc), status_code=400,\n    40\t                          form={\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body})\n    41\t    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/data-bites\", status_code=303)\n    42\t\n    43\t\n    44\tdef _edit_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, bite: sqlite3.Row, *,\n    45\t               error: str | None = None, form: dict | None = None,\n \"home_path\": \"/admin\", \"user\": user,\n    50\t         \"bite\": bite, \"status_labels\": data_bites.STATUS_LABELS,\n    51\t         \"error\": error, \"form\": form or bite},\n    52\t        status_code=status_code,\n    53\t    )\n    54\t\n    55\t\n    56\tdef _get_or_404(bite_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    57\t    bite = data_bites.get(bite_id)\n    58\t    if bite is None:\n    59\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n    60\t    return bite\n    61\t\n    62\t\n    63\t@router.get(\"/{bite_id}\")\n    64\tdef edit_form(request: Request, bite_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    65\t    return _edit_page(request, user, _get_or_404(bite_id))\n    66\t\n    67\t\n    68\t@router.post(\"/{bite_id}\", dependencies=[Depends(require_csrf)])\n    69\tdef edit_bite(request: Request, bite_id: int, user=Depends(current_user),\n    70\t              title: str = Form(\"\"), slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\")):\n    71\t    bite = _get_or_404(bite_id)\n    72\t    try:\n    73\t        data_bites.update(bite_id, title, slug, body)\n    74\t    except data_bites.DataBiteError as exc:\n    75\t        return _edit_page(request, RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/data-bites/{bite_id}\", status_code=303)\n    78\t\n    79\t\n    80\tdef _set_status(bite_id: int, status: str):\n    81\t    if not data_bites.set_status(bite_id, status):\n    82\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n    83\t    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/data-bites\", status_code=303)\n    84\t\n    85\t\n    86\t@router.post(\"/{bite_id}/publish\", dependencies=[Depends(require_csrf)])\n    87\tdef publish(bite_id: int):\n    88\t    return _set_status(bite_id, \"published\")\n    89\t\n    90\t\n    91\t@router.post(\"/{bite_id}/unpublish\", dependencies=[Depends(require_csrf)])\n    92\tdef unpublish(bite_id: int):\n    93\t    return _set_status(bite_id, \"draft\")\n    94\t\n    95\t\n    96\t@router.post(\"/{bite_id}/delete\",\n    97\t             dependencies=[Depends(require_director), Depends(require_csrf)])\n    98\tdef delete(bite_id: int):\n    99\t    if not data_bites.delete(bite_id):\n   100\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n   101\t    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/data-bites\", status_code=303)\n##### app/auth.py\n     1\t\"\"\"Sessions and CSRF.\n     2\t\n     3\tThe session is a signed cookie (Starlette's SessionMiddleware, which signs with\n     4\titsdangerous). It holds only the user's id and a CSRF token. The user is\n     5\tre-loaded from the database on every protected request, so deactivating an\n     6\taccount ends its sessions at once.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     9\t\n    10\timport secrets\n    11\timport sqlite3\n    12\t\n    13\tfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\n    14\t\n    15\tfrom app import users\n    16\t\n    17\t\n    18\tclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    19\t    \"\"\"Raised by current_user; main.py turns it into a redirect to /login.\"\"\"\n    20\t\n    21\t\n    22\tdef current_user(request: Request) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    23\t    \"\"\"Dependency: the logged-in, still-active user, or LoginRequired.\"\"\"\n    24\t    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    25\t    user = users.get_active_user(user_id) if user_id is not None else None\n    26\t    if user is None:\n    27\t        request.session.clear()\n    28\t        raise LoginRequired\n    29\t    return user\n    30\t\n    31\t\n    32\tdef require_director(user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(current_user)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    33\t    \"\"\"Dependency: current_user, but 403 unless they are a Director.\"\"\"\n    34\t    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n    35\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Directors only.\")\n    36\t    return user\n    37\t\n    38\t\n    39\tdef log_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    40\t    # A fresh session (and CSRF token) on login, so a pre-login cookie planted\n    41\t    # by someone else never becomes an authenticated one.\n    42\t    request.session.clear()\n    43\t    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n    44\t    csrf_token(request)\n    45\t\n    46\t\n    47\tdef log_out(request: Request) -> None:\n    48\t    request.session.clear()\n    49\t\n    50\t\n    51\tdef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    52\t    \"\"\"The session's CSRF token, created on first use. Templates call this.\"\"\"\n    53\t    token = request.session.get(\"csrf_token\")\n    54\t    if token is None:\n    55\t        token = request.session[\"csrf_token\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    56\t    return token\n    57\t\n    58\t\n    59\tasync def require_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    60\t    \"\"\"Dependency for every state-changing POST: the form's csrf_token must\n    61\t    match the session's.\"\"\"\n    62\t    form = await request.form()\n    63\t    submitted = form.get(\"csrf_token\")\n    64\t    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf_token\")\n    65\t    if not (isinstance(submitted, str) and expected\n    66\t            and secrets.compare_digest(submitted, expected)):\n    67\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n##### app/templating.py\n     1\t\"\"\"The Jinja environment the admin console renders with.\"\"\"\n     2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     3\t\n     4\tfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n     5\t\n     6\tfrom app import settings\n     7\tfrom app.auth import csrf_token\n     8\tfrom app.users import ROLE_LABELS\n     9\t\n    10\ttemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n    11\ttemplates.env.globals[\"csrf_token\"] = csrf_token\n    12\ttemplates.env.filters[\"role_label\"] = ROLE_LABELS.get\n##### templates/admin/_data_bite_fields.html\n     1\t  <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ action }}\">\n     2\t    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ name=\"slug\" value=\"{{ form.slug }}\" required\n     5\t      pattern=\"[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*\" aria-describedby=\"slug-hint\"></label>\n     6\t      <small id=\"slug-hint\">Lowercase letters, digits, and hyphens.</small></p>\n     7\t    <p><label>Body (Markdown)<br><textarea name=\"body\" rows=\"10\" cols=\"60\" required>{{ form.body }}</textarea></label></p>\n     8\t    <p><button
