%}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"1d244574-9068-4a60-b2e6-ff90e374bcc9","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:09:52.387Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"##### app/data_bites.py\n     1\t\"\"\"Data Bites: short, dated Markdown items.\n     2\t\n     3\tAny Analyst or Director may create, edit, publish, and unpublish any Data Bite\n     4\t(shift continuity, ADR-003); only a Director may delete one, which the routes\n     5\tenforce. Editing never changes the author or created_at.\n     6\t\n     7\tPublic-facing code reads Data Bites only through `list_published`, so a draft\n     8\tcannot leak through it.\n     9\t\"\"\"\n    10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n    11\t\n    12\timport re\n    13\timport sqlite3\n    14\t\n    15\tfrom app import db\n    16\t\n    17\tSTATUS_LABELS = {\"draft\": \"Draft\", \"published\": \"Published\"}\n    18\t_SLUG_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*\")\n    19\t_SELECT = (\"SELECT d.*, u.name AS author_name FROM data_bites d\"\n    20\t           \" JOIN users u ON u.id = d.author_id\")\n    21\t\n    22\t\n    23\tclass DataBiteError(ValueError):\n    24\t    \"\"\"A Data Bite broke a rule; the message is safe to show.\"\"\"\n    25\t\n    26\t\n    27\tclass DuplicateSlug(DataBiteError):\n    28\t    pass\n    29\t\n    30\t\n    31\tdef _validate(title: str, slug: str, body: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:\n    32\t    title, slug = title.strip(), slug.strip()\n    33\t    if not title:\n    34\t        raise DataBiteError(\"Enter a title.\")\n    35\t    if not _SLUG_PATTERN.fullmatch(slug):\n    36\t        raise DataBiteError(\"Slugs use lowercase letters, digits, and single \"\n    37\t                            \"hyphens, e.g. fall-enrollment.\")\n    38\t    if not body.strip():\n    39\t        raise DataBiteError(\"Enter a body.\")\n    40\t    return title, slug, body\n    41\t\n    42\t\n    43\tdef _duplicate_slug_error(exc: sqlite3.IntegrityError, slug: str) -> DataBiteError:\n    44\t    \"\"\"Translate the slug's UNIQUE violation; re-raise any other constraint.\"\"\"\n    45\t    if \"data_bites.slug\" not in str(exc):\n    46\t        raise exc\n    47\t    return DuplicateSlug(f\"Another Data Bite already uses the slug {slug}.\")\n    48\t\n    49\t\n    50\tdef create(title: str, slug: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    51\t    \"\"\"Create a draft Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    52\t    title, slug, body = _validate(title, slug, body)\n    53\t    try:\n    54\t        with db.connect() as conn:\n    55\t            cursor = conn.execute(\n    56\t                \"INSERT INTO data_bites (title, slug, body, author_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n    57\t                (title, slug, body, author_id),\n    58\t            )\n    59\t    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n    60\t        raise _duplicate_slug_error(exc, slug) from None\n    61\t    return cursor.lastrowid\n    62\t\n    63\t\n    64\tdef update(bite_id: int, title: str, slug: str, body: str) -> bool:\n    65\t    \"\"\"Edit title, slug, and body. The author, created_at, and status are kept.\n    66\t    False if there is no such Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    67\t    title, slug, body = _validate(title, slug, body)\n    68\t    try:\n    69\t        with db.connect() as conn:\n    70\t            return conn.execute(\n    71\t                \"UPDATE data_bites SET title = ?, slug = ?, body = ?,\"\n    72\t                \" updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?\",\n    73\t                (title, slug, body, bite_id),\n    74\t            ).rowcount == 1\n    75\t    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n    76\t        raise _duplicate_slug_error(exc, slug) from None\n    77\t\n    78\t\n    79\tdef get(bite_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    80\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n    81\t        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} WHERE d.id = ?\", (bite_id,)).fetchone()\n    82\t\n    83\t\n    84\tdef list_all() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    85\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n    86\t        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} ORDER BY d.updated_at DESC, d.id DESC\").fetchall()\n    87\t\n    88\t\n    89\tdef set_status(bite_id: int, status: str) -> bool:\n    90\t    \"\"\"Publish or unpublish. False if there is no such Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    91\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n    92\t        return conn.execute(\n    93\t            \"UPDATE data_bites SET status = ?, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?\",\n    94\t            (status, bite_id),\n    95\t        ).rowcount == 1\n    96\t\n    97\t\n    98\tdef delete(bite_id: int) -> bool:\n    99\t    \"\"\"False if there is no such Data Bite.\"\"\"\n   100\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n   101\t        return conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM data_bites WHERE id = ?\", (bite_id,)).rowcount == 1\n   102\t\n   103\t\n   104\tdef list_published() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n   105\t    \"\"\"The public-facing query: published Data Bites only, newest first.\"\"\"\n   106\t    with db.connect() as conn:\n   107\t        return conn.execute(\n   108\t            f\"{_SELECT} WHERE d.status = 'published' ORDER BY d.created_at DESC, d.id DESC\"\n   109\t        ).fetchall()\n##### app/db.py\n     1\t\"\"\"SQLite access. Every table's schema lives here, so init_db() builds them all.\"\"\"\n     2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     3\t\n     4\timport sqlite3\n     5\tfrom collections.abc import Iterator\n     6\tfrom contextlib import contextmanager\n     7\t\n     8\tfrom app import settings\n     9\t\n    10\tSCHEMA = \"\"\"\n    11\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    12\t    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    13\t    email         TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    14\t    name          TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    15\t    role          TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    16\t    password_hash TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    17\t    is_active     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    18\t    created_at    TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n    19\t);\n    20\t\n    21\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS data_bites (\n    22\t    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    23\t    title      TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    24\t    slug       TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    25\t    body       TEXT    NOT NULL,  -- raw Markdown; sanitized only when rendered\n    26\t    status     TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    27\t    author_id  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    28\t    created_at TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),\n    29\t    updated_at TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n    30\t);\n    31\t\"\"\"\n    32\t\n    33\t\n    34\t@contextmanager\n    35\tdef connect() -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    36\t    \"\"\"Open the database named by settings (read at call time, so tests can swap\n    37\t    it). Commits on success, rolls back on error, always closes.\"\"\"\n    38\t    conn = sqlite3.connect(settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    39\t    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    40\t    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n    41\t    try:\n    42\t        with conn:\n    43\t            yield conn\n    44\t    finally:\n    45\t        conn.close()\n    46\t\n    47\t\n    48\tdef init_db() -> None:\n    49\t    with connect() as conn:\n    50\t        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n##### app/main.py\n     1\t\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n     2\t\n     3\tThe admin console lives under /admin (login required) and the public preview\n     4\tat /. Routes live in their own modules under app/routes/ and are included here.\n     5\tKeep this file small.\n     6\t\"\"\"\n     7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     8\t\n     9\timport sys\n    10\tfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\n    11\t\n    12\tfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, HTTPException, Request\n    13\tfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n    14\tfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n    15\t\n    16\tfrom app import db, settings\n    17\tfrom app.auth import LoginRequired, current_user\n    18\tfrom app.data_bites import list_published as published_data_bites\n    19\tfrom app.routes import admin, auth, data_bites, users\n    20\tfrom app.templating import templates\n    21\t\n    22\tSESSION_MAX_AGE = 8 * 60 * 60  # one working day\n    23\tALL_METHODS = [\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"PATCH\", \"DELETE\", \"OPTIONS\"]\n    24\t\n    25\t\n    26\t@asynccontextmanager\n    27\tasync def lifespan(app: FastAPI):\n    28\t    # At server start, not import, so importing app.main never touches a database.\n    29\t    db.init_db()\n    30\t    if settings.SECRET_KEY_IS_PLACEHOLDER:\n    31\t        print(\"WARNING: CMS_SECRET_KEY is unset or the .env.example placeholder; \"\n    32\t              \"session cookies can be forged. Set a long random value in .env.\",\n    33\t              file=sys.stderr)\n    34\t    yield\n    35\t\n    36\t\n    37\tdef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    38\t    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\", lifespan=lifespan)\n    39\t    app.add_middleware(\n    40\t        SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n    41\t        session_cookie=\"cms_session\", max_age=SESSION_MAX_AGE, same_site=\"lax\",\n    42\t    )\n    43\t\n    44\t    @app.exception_handler(LoginRequired)\n    45\t    def redirect_to_login(request: Request, exc: LoginRequired):\n    46\t        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    47\t\n    48\t    @app.get(\"/\")\n    49\t    def public_home(request: Request):\n    50\t        # Titles only: the public Data Bite pages (and links to them) arrive\n    51\t        # with the public-site ticket, T07.\n    52\t        items = [{\"title\": b[\"title\"]} for b in published_data_bites()]\n    53\t        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n    54\t            request, \"public/home.html\",\n    55\t            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": items},\n    56\t        )\n    57\t\n    58\t    app.include_router(auth.router)\n    59\t    app.include_router(admin.router)\n    60\t    app.include_router(users.router)\n    61\t    app.include_router(data_bites.router)\n    62\t\n    63\t    # Must stay last: any /admin request no router above claimed, by path or by\n    64\t    # method. Anonymous visitors are sent to login (so they can't probe which\n    65\t    # admin paths exist); signed-in users get 405/404.\n    66\t    @app.api_route(\"/admin\", methods=ALL_METHODS,\n    67\t                   dependencies=[Depends(current_user)], include_in_schema=False)\n    68\t    def admin_method_not_allowed():\n    69\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=405)\n    70\t\n    71\t    @app.api_route(\"/admin/{rest:path}\", methods=ALL_METHODS,\n    72\t                   dependencies=[Depends(current_user)], include_in_schema=False)\n    73\t    def admin_not_found(rest: str):\n    74\t        if rest == \"\":\n    75\t            return RedirectResponse(\"/admin\", status_code=307)\n    76\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n    77\t\n    78\t    return app\n    79\t\n    80\t\n    81\tapp = create_app()\n##### app/routes/data_bites.py\n     1\t\"\"\"Data Bites in the admin console. Any signed-in user may do everything here\n     2\texcept delete, which is Director only.\"\"\"\n     3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     4\t\n     5\timport sqlite3\n     6\t\n     7\tfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\n     8\tfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n     9\t\n    10\tfrom app import data_bites\n    11\tfrom app.auth import current_user, require_csrf, require_director\n    12\tfrom app.templating import templates\n    13\t\n    14\trouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/data-bites\", dependencies=[Depends(current_user)])\n    15\t\n    16\t\n    17\tdef _list_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, *, error: str | None = None,\n    18\t               form: dict | None = None, status_code: int = 200):\n    19\t    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n    20\t        request, \"admin/data_bites.html\",\n    21\t        {\"title\": \"Data Bites\", \"home_path\": \"/admin\", \"user\": user,\n    22\t         \"bites\": data_bites.list_all(), \"status_labels\": data_bites.STATUS_LABELS,\n    23\t         \"error\": error, \"form\": form or {}},\n    24\t        status_code=status_code,\n    25\t    )\n    26\t\n    27\t\n    28\t@router.get(\"\")\n    29\tdef list_bites(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    30\t    return _list_page(request, user)\n    31\t\n    32\t\n    33\t@router.post(\"\", dependencies=[Depends(require_csrf)])\n    34\tdef create_bite(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n    35\t                slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\")):\n    36\t    try:\n    37\t        data_bites.create(title, slug, body, user[\"id\"])\n    38\t    except data_bites.DataBiteError as exc:\n    39\t        return _list_page(request, user, error=str(exc), status_code=400,\n    40\t                          form={\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body})\n    41\t    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/data-bites\", status_code=303)\n    42\t\n    43\t\n    44\tdef _edit_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, bite: sqlite3.Row, *,\n    45\t               error: str | None = None, form: dict | None = None,\n    46\t               status_code: int = 200):\n    47\t    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n    48\t        request, \"admin/data_bite_edit.html\",\n    49\t        {\"title\": \"Edit Data Bite\", \"home_path\": \"/admin\", \"user\": user,\n    50\t         \"bite\": bite, \"status_labels\": data_bites.STATUS_LABELS,\n    51\t         \"error\": error, \"form\": form or bite},\n    52\t        status_code=status_code,\n    53\t    )\n    54\t\n    55\t\n    56\tdef _get_or_404(bite_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    57\t    bite = data_bites.get(bite_id)\n    58\t    if bite is None:\n    59\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n    60\t    return bite\n    61\t\n    62\t\n    63\t@router.get(\"/{bite_id}\")\n    64\tdef edit_form(request: Request, bite_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    65\t    return _edit_page(request, user, _get_or_404(bite_id))\n    66\t\n    67\t\n    68\t@router.post(\"/{bite_id}\", dependencies=[Depends(require_csrf)])\n    69\tdef edit_bite(request: Request, bite_id: int, user=Depends(current_user),\n    70\t              title: str = Form(\"\"), slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\")):\n    71\t    bite = _get_or_404(bite_id)\n    72\t    try:\n    73\t        data_bites.update(bite_id, title, slug, body)\n    74\t    except data_bites.DataBiteError as exc:\n    75\t        return _edit_page(request, user, bite, error=str(exc), status_code=400,\n    76\t                          form={\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body})\n    77\t    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/data-bites/{bite_id}\", status_code=303)\n    78\t\n    79\t\n    80\tdef _set_status(bite_id: int, status: str):\n    81\t    if not data_bites.set_status(bite_id, status):\n    82\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n    83\t    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/data-bites\", status_code=303)\n    84\t\n    85\t\n    86\t@router.post(\"/{bite_id}/publish\", dependencies=[Depends(require_csrf)])\n    87\tdef publish(bite_id: int):\n    88\t    return _set_status(bite_id, \"published\")\n    89\t\n    90\t\n    91\t@router.post(\"/{bite_id}/unpublish\", dependencies=[Depends(require_csrf)])\n    92\tdef unpublish(bite_id: int):\n    93\t    return _set_status(bite_id, \"draft\")\n    94\t\n    95\t\n    96\t@router.post(\"/{bite_id}/delete\",\n    97\t             dependencies=[Depends(require_director), Depends(require_csrf)])\n    98\tdef delete(bite_id: int):\n    99\t    if not data_bites.delete(bite_id):\n   100\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n   101\t    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/data-bites\", status_code=303)\n##### app/auth.py\n     1\t\"\"\"Sessions and CSRF.\n     2\t\n     3\tThe session is a signed cookie (Starlette's SessionMiddleware, which signs with\n     4\titsdangerous). It holds only the user's id and a CSRF token. The user is\n     5\tre-loaded from the database on every protected request, so deactivating an\n     6\taccount ends its sessions at once.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     9\t\n    10\timport secrets\n    11\timport sqlite3\n    12\t\n    13\tfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\n    14\t\n    15\tfrom app import users\n    16\t\n    17\t\n    18\tclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    19\t    \"\"\"Raised by current_user; main.py turns it into a redirect to /login.\"\"\"\n    20\t\n    21\t\n    22\tdef current_user(request: Request) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    23\t    \"\"\"Dependency: the logged-in, still-active user, or LoginRequired.\"\"\"\n    24\t    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    25\t    user = users.get_active_user(user_id) if user_id is not None else None\n    26\t    if user is None:\n    27\t        request.session.clear()\n    28\t        raise LoginRequired\n    29\t    return user\n    30\t\n    31\t\n    32\tdef require_director(user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(current_user)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    33\t    \"\"\"Dependency: current_user, but 403 unless they are a Director.\"\"\"\n    34\t    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n    35\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Directors only.\")\n    36\t    return user\n    37\t\n    38\t\n    39\tdef log_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    40\t    # A fresh session (and CSRF token) on login, so a pre-login cookie planted\n    41\t    # by someone else never becomes an authenticated one.\n    42\t    request.session.clear()\n    43\t    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n    44\t    csrf_token(request)\n    45\t\n    46\t\n    47\tdef log_out(request: Request) -> None:\n    48\t    request.session.clear()\n    49\t\n    50\t\n    51\tdef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    52\t    \"\"\"The session's CSRF token, created on first use. Templates call this.\"\"\"\n    53\t    token = request.session.get(\"csrf_token\")\n    54\t    if token is None:\n    55\t        token = request.session[\"csrf_token\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    56\t    return token\n    57\t\n    58\t\n    59\tasync def require_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    60\t    \"\"\"Dependency for every state-changing POST: the form's csrf_token must\n    61\t    match the session's.\"\"\"\n    62\t    form = await request.form()\n    63\t    submitted = form.get(\"csrf_token\")\n    64\t    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf_token\")\n    65\t    if not (isinstance(submitted, str) and expected\n    66\t            and secrets.compare_digest(submitted, expected)):\n    67\t        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n##### app/templating.py\n     1\t\"\"\"The Jinja environment the admin console renders with.\"\"\"\n     2\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     3\t\n     4\tfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n     5\t\n     6\tfrom app import settings\n     7\tfrom app.auth import csrf_token\n     8\tfrom app.users import ROLE_LABELS\n     9\t\n    10\ttemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n    11\ttemplates.env.globals[\"csrf_token\"] = csrf_token\n    12\ttemplates.env.filters[\"role_label\"] = ROLE_LABELS.get\n##### templates/admin/_data_bite_fields.html\n     1\t  <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ action }}\">\n     2\t    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n     3\t    <p><label>Title <input type=\"text\" name=\"title\" value=\"{{ form.title }}\" required></label></p>\n     4\t    <p><label>Slug <input type=\"text\" name=\"slug\" value=\"{{ form.slug }}\" required\n     5\t      pattern=\"[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*\" aria-describedby=\"slug-hint\"></label>\n     6\t      <small id=\"slug-hint\">Lowercase letters, digits, and hyphens.</small></p>\n     7\t    <p><label>Body (Markdown)<br><textarea name=\"body\" rows=\"10\" cols=\"60\" required>{{ form.body }}</textarea></label></p>\n     8\t    <p><button type=\"submit\">{{ submit }}</button></p>\n     9\t  </form>\n##### templates/admin/data_bite_edit.html\n     1\t{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n     2\t{% block content %}\n     3\t  <p><a href=\"/admin/data-bites\">All Data Bites</a></p>\n     4\t  <h1>Edit Data Bite</h1>\n     5\t  <p>{{ status_labels[bite.status] }}. Created {{ bite.created_at }} by {{ bite.author_name }}.\n     6\t     Last updated {{ bite.updated_at }}.</p>\n     7\t  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n     8\t  {% set action = \"/admin/data-bites/\" ~ bite.id %}\n     9\t  {% set submit = \"Save changes\" %}\n    10\t  {% include \"admin/_data_bite_fields.html\" %}\n    11\t{% endblock %}\n##### templates/admin/data_bites.html\n     1\t{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n     2\t{% block content %}\n     3\t  <h1>Data Bites</h1>\n     4\t  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n     5\t  {% if bites %}\n     6\t  <table>\n     7\t    <thead><tr><th>Title</th><th>Slug</th><th>Status</th><th>Author</th><th>Updated</th><th></th></tr></thead>\n     8\t    <tbody>\n     9\t    {% for b in bites %}\n    10\t      <tr id=\"data-bite-{{ b.id }}\">\n    11\t        <td><a href=\"/admin/data-bites/{{ b.id }}\">{{ b.title }}</a></td>\n    12\t        <td><code>{{ b.slug }}</code></td>\n    13\t        <td>{{ status_labels[b.status] }}</td>\n    14\t        <td>{{ b.author_name }}</td>\n    15\t        <td>{{ b.updated_at }}</td>\n    16\t        <td>\n    17\t          {% set action = \"unpublish\" if b.status == \"published\" else \"publish\" %}\n    18\t          <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/data-bites/{{ b.id }}/{{ action }}\">\n    19\t            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n    20\t            <button type=\"submit\">{{ action | capitalize }}</button>\n    21\t          </form>\n    22\t          {% if user.role == \"admin\" %}\n    23\t          <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/data-bites/{{ b.id }}/delete\">\n    24\t            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n    25\t            <button type=\"submit\">Delete</button>\n    26\t          </form>\n    27\t          {% endif %}\n    28\t        </td>\n    29\t      </tr>\n    30\t    {% endfor %}\n    31\t    </tbody>\n    32\t  </table>\n    33\t  {% else %}\n    34\t  <p>No Data Bites yet.</p>\n    35\t  {% endif %}\n    36\t\n    37\t  <h2>New Data Bite</h2>\n    38\t  {% set action = \"/admin/data-bites\" %}\n    39\t  {% set submit = \"Save draft\" %}\n    40\t  {% include \"admin/_data_bite_fields.html\" %}\n    41\t{% endblock %}\n##### templates/admin/home.html\n     1\t{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n     2\t{% block content %}\n     3\t  <h1>Admin console</h1>\n     4\t  <p>Signed in as {{ user.email }} ({{ user.role | role_label }}).</p>\n     5\t  <p><a href=\"/admin/data-bites\">Data Bites</a></p>\n     6\t  {% if user.role == \"admin\" %}\n     7\t    <p><a href=\"/admin/users\">Manage users</a></p>\n     8\t  {% endif %}\n     9\t  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/logout\">\n    10\t    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n    11\t    <button type=\"submit\">Log out</button>\n    12\t  </form>\n    13\t{% endblock %}\n##### templates/public/home.html\n     1\t{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n     2\t{% block content %}\n     3\t  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n     4\t  {% if items %}\n     5\t    <ul>{% for item in items %}<li>{% if item.href %}<a href=\"{{ item.href }}\">{{ item.title }}</a>{% else %}{{ item.title }}{% endif %}</li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n     6\t  {% else %}\n     7\t test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n     8\t        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n     9\t\"\"\"\n    10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n    11\t\n    12\timport re\n    13\t\n    14\timport pytest\n    15\tfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n    16\t\n    17\tfrom app import db, settings, users\n    18\tfrom app.main import create_app\n    19\t\n    20\t# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n    21\t# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\n    22\tDEMO_USERS = {\n    23\t    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"[REDACTED]\"},\n    24\t    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"[REDACTED]\"},\n    25\t}\n    26\t\n    27\t\n    28\tdef csrf_from(html: str) -> str:\n    29\t    \"\"\"Pull the CSRF token out of a rendered form.\"\"\"\n    30\t    match = re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', html)\n    31\t    assert match, \"no csrf_token field in the page\"\n    32\t    return match.group(1)\n    33\t\n    34\t\n    35\tdef post_form(c: TestClient, path: str, data: dict, *, with_csrf=True):\n    36\t    \"\"\"POST an admin form as the logged-in client, with the session's CSRF token.\"\"\"\n    37\t    if with_csrf:\n    38\t        data = {**data, \"csrf_token\": csrf_from(c.get(\"/admin\").text)}\n    39\t    return c.post(path, data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n    40\t\n    41\t\n    42\tdef user_row(director: TestClient, email: str) -> str:\n    43\t    \"\"\"This user's row of the Director's user list, as HTML.\"\"\"\n    44\t    for row in director.get(\"/admin/users\").text.split(\"<tr\")[1:]:\n    45\t        if email in row:\n    46\t            return row\n    47\t    raise AssertionError(f\"{email} is not listed\")\n    48\t\n    49\t\n    50\tdef user_id(director: TestClient, email: str) -> str:\n    51\t    return re.match(r' id=\"user-(\\d+)\"', user_row(director, email)).group(1)\n    52\t\n    53\t\n    54\t@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\n    55\tdef seeded_db(tmp_path, monkeypatch):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Every test gets its own database holding the DEMO_USERS.\"\"\"\n    57\t    monkeypatch.setattr(settings, \"DATABASE_PATH\", tmp_path / \"test.db\")\n    58\t    db.init_db()\n    59\t    for role, user in DEMO_USERS.items():\n    60\t        users.create_user(user[\"email\"], user[\"password\"], role)\n    61\t    return settings.DATABASE_PATH\n    62\t\n    63\t\n    64\t@pytest.fixture\n    65\tdef client() -> TestClient:\n    66\t    return TestClient(create_app())\n    67\t\n    68\t\n    69\t@pytest.fixture\n    70\tdef client_as():\n    71\t    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n    72\t\n    73\t    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n    74\t        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n    75\t        c = TestClient(create_app())\n    76\t        form = c.get(\"/login\")\n    77\t        if form.status_code == 404:\n    78\t            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n    79\t        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n    80\t                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"],\n    81\t                                          \"csrf_token\": csrf_from(form.text)},\n    82\t                          follow_redirects=False)\n    83\t        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n    84\t            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n    85\t        return c\n    86\t\n    87\t    return _login\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return {"parentUuid":"9f72e203-c067-4491-9269-efbbfed30d85","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWbsNVQcTGQBw5fBHT3L","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01ERSRBLGhQDFCCvbvTefmJj","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t04_reports.py","content":"\"\"\"T04: Reports, draft/publish lockdown (issue #5).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom tests.conftest import post_form, second_analyst\n\nREPORT = {\"title\": \"Factbook 2026\", \"slug\": \"factbook\",\n          \"body\": \"Admissions, enrollment, and **program** statistics.\"}\n\n\ndef report_row(c: TestClient, slug: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"This Report's row of the admin list, as HTML.\"\"\"\n    for row in c.get(\"/admin/reports\").text.split(\"<tr\")[1:]:\n        if f\"<code>{slug}</code>\" in row:\n            return row\n    raise AssertionError(f\"{slug} is not listed\")\n\n\ndef report_id(c: TestClient, slug: str) -> str:\n    return re.match(r' id=\"report-(\\d+)\"', report_row(c, slug)).group(1)\n\n\ndef create_report(c: TestClient, **override) -> str:\n    response = post_form(c, \"/admin/reports\", {**REPORT, **override})\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return report_id(c, override.get(\"slug\", REPORT[\"slug\"]))\n\n\ndef published_report(director: TestClient, analyst: TestClient) -> str:\n    \"\"\"An Analyst-drafted Report that the Director has published.\"\"\"\n    rid = create_report(analyst)\n    assert post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/publish\", {}).status_code == 303\n    return rid\n\n\ndef test_analyst_creates_a_draft_report(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = post_form(analyst, \"/admin/reports\", REPORT)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/admin/reports\"\n    row = report_row(analyst, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] in row and \"Draft\" in row\n\n\ndef test_a_report_requires_a_markdown_body(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = post_form(analyst, \"/admin/reports\", {**REPORT, \"body\": \"   \"})\n    assert response.status_code == 400\n    assert \"Enter a body.\" in response.text\n    assert f\"<code>{REPORT['slug']}</code>\" not in analyst.get(\"/admin/reports\").text\n\n\ndef test_any_analyst_edits_any_draft_report_and_the_author_is_kept(client_as):\n    riley = second_analyst(client_as(\"admin\"))\n    rid = create_report(riley)\n\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")  # not the author\n    edited = {\"title\": \"Factbook 2026 (draft 2)\", \"slug\": \"factbook\",\n              \"body\": \"Now with *diversity* tables.\"}\n    response = post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}\", edited)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n\n    page = analyst.get(f\"/admin/reports/{rid}\").text\n    assert edited[\"title\"] in page and edited[\"body\"] in page\n    assert \"by Riley Intern\" in page\n\n\ndef test_analyst_cannot_publish_a_report(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = create_report(analyst)\n    assert post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/publish\", {}).status_code == 403\n    assert \"Draft\" in report_row(analyst, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n    assert \"/publish\" not in analyst.get(\"/admin/reports\").text\n\n\ndef test_analyst_cannot_delete_a_draft_report(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = create_report(analyst)\n    assert post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/delete\", {}).status_code == 403\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] in report_row(analyst, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n    assert \"/delete\" not in analyst.get(\"/admin/reports\").text\n\n\ndef test_published_report_is_locked_against_analyst_edit_unpublish_and_delete(client_as):\n    director, analyst = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = published_report(director, analyst)\n\n    edit = post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}\",\n                     {**REPORT, \"title\": \"Tampered\", \"body\": \"Tampered.\"})\n    assert edit.status_code == 403\n    assert post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/unpublish\", {}).status_code == 403\n    assert post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/delete\", {}).status_code == 403\n\n    row = report_row(director, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] in row and \"Published\" in row\n    assert \"Tampered\" not in director.get(f\"/admin/reports/{rid}\").text\n\n\ndef test_analyst_sees_a_published_report_read_only(client_as):\n    director, analyst = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = published_report(director, analyst)\n    page = analyst.get(f\"/admin/reports/{rid}\")\n    assert page.status_code == 200\n    assert REPORT[\"body\"] in page.text\n    assert f'action=\"/admin/reports/{rid}\"' not in page.text\n    assert \"Only the Director\" in page.text\n\n\ndef test_director_publishes_edits_unpublishes_and_deletes_a_report(client_as):\n    director, analyst = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = published_report(director, analyst)\n    assert \"Published\" in report_row(director, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n\n    edited = {**REPORT, \"title\": \"Factbook 2026 (corrected)\"}\n    assert post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}\", edited).status_code == 303\n    row = report_row(director, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n    assert edited[\"title\"] in row and \"Published\" in row\n\n    assert post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/unpublish\", {}).status_code == 303\n    assert \"Draft\" in report_row(director, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n\n    # Back in draft, an Analyst can work on it again.\n    assert post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}\", REPORT).status_code == 303\n\n    assert post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/delete\", {}).status_code == 303\n    assert REPORT[\"slug\"] not in director.get(\"/admin/reports\").text\n    assert post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/delete\", {}).status_code == 404\n\n\ndef test_director_deletes_a_published_report(client_as):\n    director, analyst = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = published_report(director, analyst)\n    assert post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/delete\", {}).status_code == 303\n    assert REPORT[\"slug\"] not in director.get(\"/admin/reports\").text\n\n\ndef test_duplicate_report_slug_is_rejected_with_a_validation_error(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    create_report(analyst)\n    response = post_form(analyst, \"/admin/reports\", {**REPORT, \"title\": \"Another\"})\n    assert response.status_code == 400\n    assert f\"Another Report already uses the slug {REPORT['slug']}\" in response.text\n    assert analyst.get(\"/admin/reports\").text.count(f\"<code>{REPORT['slug']}</code>\") == 1\n\n\ndef test_editing_to_another_reports_slug_is_rejected(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    create_report(analyst)\n    other = create_report(analyst, slug=\"common-data-set\", title=\"Common Data Set\")\n    response = post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{other}\",\n                         {**REPORT, \"title\": \"Common Data Set\"})\n    assert response.status_code == 400\n    assert f\"already uses the slug {REPORT['slug']}\" in response.text\n    assert report_id(analyst, \"common-data-set\") == other\n\n\ndef test_a_data_bite_may_share_a_slug_with_a_report(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    create_report(analyst)\n    bite = {\"title\": \"Factbook is out\", \"slug\": REPORT[\"slug\"], \"body\": \"See the Factbook.\"}\n    assert post_form(analyst, \"/admin/data-bites\", bite).status_code == 303\n    assert f\"<code>{REPORT['slug']}</code>\" in analyst.get(\"/admin/data-bites\").text\n    assert f\"<code>{REPORT['slug']}</code>\" in analyst.get(\"/admin/reports\").text\n\n\ndef test_a_draft_report_is_never_shown_publicly(client_as, client):\n    director, analyst = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = published_report(director, analyst)\n    create_report(analyst, slug=\"cds-2027\", title=\"Unreleased CDS\")\n\n    home = client.get(\"/\").text\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] in home\n    assert \"Unreleased CDS\" not in home and \"cds-2027\" not in home\n\n    post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/unpublish\", {})\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] not in client.get(\"/\").text\n\n\n# Every Report route. {id} is filled with an existing draft Report's id.\nROUTES = [\n    (\"GET\", \"/admin/reports\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports\", {**REPORT, \"slug\": \"new-slug\"}),\n    (\"GET\", \"/admin/reports/{id}\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports/{id}\", {**REPORT, \"title\": \"Changed title\"}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports/{id}/publish\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports/{id}/unpublish\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports/{id}/delete\", {}),\n]\nPOST_ROUTES = [r for r in ROUTES if r[0] == \"POST\"]\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef existing_report(client_as):\n    return create_report(client_as(\"editor\"))\n\n\ndef assert_unchanged(c: TestClient):\n    \"\"\"The Report from `existing_report` is still the only one, still a draft,\n    with its original title.\"\"\"\n    assert c.get(\"/admin/reports\").text.count('id=\"report-') == 1\n    row = report_row(c, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] in row and \"Draft\" in row\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"method, path, data\", ROUTES)\ndef path.format(id=existing_report),\n                              data=data or None, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303, 307)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"].endswith(\"/login\")\n    assert_unchanged(client_as(\"admin\"))\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"method, path, data\", POST_ROUTES)\ndef test_report_posts_without_csrf_token_are_rejected(\n        client_as, existing_report, method, path, data):\n    director = client_as(\"admin\")\n    director.get(\"/admin\")  # the session has a token; the POST just omits it\n    response = post_form(director, path.format(id=existing_report), data, with_csrf=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n    assert_unchanged(director)\n\n\ndef test_every_signed_in_user_sees_the_reports_link(client_as):\n    for role in (\"admin\", \"editor\"):\n        assert 'href=\"/admin/reports\"' in client_as(role).get(\"/admin\").text\n\n\ndef test_missing_report_is_404(client_as):\n    director = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert director.get(\"/admin/reports/999\").status_code == 404\n Reports, draft/publish lockdown (issue #5).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom tests.conftest import post_form, second_analyst\n\nREPORT = {\"title\": \"Factbook 2026\", \"slug\": \"factbook\",\n          \"body\": \"Admissions, enrollment, and **program** statistics.\"}\n\n\ndef report_row(c: TestClient, slug: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"This Report's row of the admin list, as HTML.\"\"\"\n    for row in c.get(\"/admin/reports\").text.split(\"<tr\")[1:]:\n        if f\"<code>{slug}</code>\" in row:\n            return row\n    raise AssertionError(f\"{slug} is not listed\")\n\n\ndef report_id(c: TestClient, slug: str) -> str:\n    return re.match(r' id=\"report-(\\d+)\"', report_row(c, slug)).group(1)\n\n\ndef create_report(c: TestClient, **override) -> str:\n    response = post_form(c, \"/admin/reports\", {**REPORT, **override})\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return report_id(c, override.get(\"slug\", REPORT[\"slug\"]))\n\n\ndef published_report(director: TestClient, analyst: TestClient) -> str:\n    \"\"\"An Analyst-drafted Report that the Director has published.\"\"\"\n    rid = create_report(analyst)\n    assert post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/publish\", {}).status_code == 303\n    return rid\n\n\ndef test_analyst_creates_a_draft_report(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = post_form(analyst, \"/admin/reports\", REPORT)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/admin/reports\"\n    row = report_row(analyst, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] in row and \"Draft\" in row\n\n\ndef test_a_report_requires_a_markdown_body(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = post_form(analyst, \"/admin/reports\", {**REPORT, \"body\": \"   \"})\n    assert response.status_code == 400\n    assert \"Enter a body.\" in response.text\n    assert f\"<code>{REPORT['slug']}</code>\" not in analyst.get(\"/admin/reports\").text\n\n\ndef test_any_analyst_edits_any_draft_report_and_the_author_is_kept(client_as):\n    riley = second_analyst(client_as(\"admin\"))\n    rid = create_report(riley)\n\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")  # not the author\n    edited = {\"title\": \"Factbook 2026 (draft 2)\", \"slug\": \"factbook\",\n              \"body\": \"Now with *diversity* tables.\"}\n    response = post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}\", edited)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n\n    page = analyst.get(f\"/admin/reports/{rid}\").text\n    assert edited[\"title\"] in page and edited[\"body\"] in page\n    assert \"by Riley Intern\" in page\n\n\ndef test_analyst_cannot_publish_a_report(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = create_report(analyst)\n    assert post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/publish\", {}).status_code == 403\n    assert \"Draft\" in report_row(analyst, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n    assert \"/publish\" not in analyst.get(\"/admin/reports\").text\n\n\ndef test_analyst_cannot_delete_a_draft_report(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = create_report(analyst)\n    assert post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/delete\", {}).status_code == 403\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] in report_row(analyst, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n    assert \"/delete\" not in analyst.get(\"/admin/reports\").text\n\n\ndef test_published_report_is_locked_against_analyst_edit_unpublish_and_delete(client_as):\n    director, analyst = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = published_report(director, analyst)\n\n    edit = post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}\",\n                     {**REPORT, \"title\": \"Tampered\", \"body\": \"Tampered.\"})\n    assert edit.status_code == 403\n    assert post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/unpublish\", {}).status_code == 403\n    assert post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/delete\", {}).status_code == 403\n\n    row = report_row(director, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] in row and \"Published\" in row\n    assert \"Tampered\" not in director.get(f\"/admin/reports/{rid}\").text\n\n\ndef test_analyst_sees_a_published_report_read_only(client_as):\n    director, analyst = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = published_report(director, analyst)\n    page = analyst.get(f\"/admin/reports/{rid}\")\n    assert page.status_code == 200\n    assert REPORT[\"body\"] in page.text\n    assert f'action=\"/admin/reports/{rid}\"' not in page.text\n    assert \"Only the Director\" in page.text\n\n\ndef test_director_publishes_edits_unpublishes_and_deletes_a_report(client_as):\n    director, analyst = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = published_report(director, analyst)\n    assert \"Published\" in report_row(director, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n\n    edited = {**REPORT, \"title\": \"Factbook 2026 (corrected)\"}\n    assert post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}\", edited).status_code == 303\n    row = report_row(director, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n    assert edited[\"title\"] in row and \"Published\" in row\n\n    assert post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/unpublish\", {}).status_code == 303\n    assert \"Draft\" in report_row(director, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n\n    # Back in draft, an Analyst can work on it again.\n    assert post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}\", REPORT).status_code == 303\n\n    assert post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/delete\", {}).status_code == 303\n    assert REPORT[\"slug\"] not in director.get(\"/admin/reports\").text\n    assert post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/delete\", {}).status_code == 404\n\n\ndef test_director_deletes_a_published_report(client_as):\n    director, analyst = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = published_report(director, analyst)\n    assert post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/delete\", {}).status_code == 303\n    assert REPORT[\"slug\"] not in director.get(\"/admin/reports\").text\n\n\ndef test_duplicate_report_slug_is_rejected_with_a_validation_error(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    create_report(analyst)\n    response = post_form(analyst, \"/admin/reports\", {**REPORT, \"title\": \"Another\"})\n    assert response.status_code == 400\n    assert f\"Another Report already uses the slug {REPORT['slug']}\" in response.text\n    assert analyst.get(\"/admin/reports\").text.count(f\"<code>{REPORT['slug']}</code>\") == 1\n\n\ndef test_editing_to_another_reports_slug_is_rejected(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    create_report(analyst)\n    other = create_report(analyst, slug=\"common-data-set\", title=\"Common Data Set\")\n    response = post_form(analyst, f\"/admin/reports/{other}\",\n                         {**REPORT, \"title\": \"Common Data Set\"})\n    assert response.status_code == 400\n    assert f\"already uses the slug {REPORT['slug']}\" in response.text\n    assert report_id(analyst, \"common-data-set\") == other\n\n\ndef test_a_data_bite_may_share_a_slug_with_a_report(client_as):\n    analyst = client_as(\"editor\")\n    create_report(analyst)\n    bite = {\"title\": \"Factbook is out\", \"slug\": REPORT[\"slug\"], \"body\": \"See the Factbook.\"}\n    assert post_form(analyst, \"/admin/data-bites\", bite).status_code == 303\n    assert f\"<code>{REPORT['slug']}</code>\" in analyst.get(\"/admin/data-bites\").text\n    assert f\"<code>{REPORT['slug']}</code>\" in analyst.get(\"/admin/reports\").text\n\n\ndef test_a_draft_report_is_never_shown_publicly(client_as, client):\n    director, analyst = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = published_report(director, analyst)\n    create_report(analyst, slug=\"cds-2027\", title=\"Unreleased CDS\")\n\n    home = client.get(\"/\").text\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] in home\n    assert \"Unreleased CDS\" not in home and \"cds-2027\" not in home\n\n    post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/unpublish\", {})\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] not in client.get(\"/\").text\n\n\n# Every Report route. {id} is filled with an existing draft Report's id.\nROUTES = [\n    (\"GET\", \"/admin/reports\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports\", {**REPORT, \"slug\": \"new-slug\"}),\n    (\"GET\", \"/admin/reports/{id}\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports/{id}\", {**REPORT, \"title\": \"Changed title\"}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports/{id}/publish\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports/{id}/unpublish\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports/{id}/delete\", {}),\n]\nPOST_ROUTES = [r for r in ROUTES if r[0] == \"POST\"]\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef existing_report(client_as):\n    return create_report(client_as(\"editor\"))\n\n\ndef assert_unchanged(c: TestClient):\n    \"\"\"The Report from `existing_report` is still the only one, still a draft,\n    with its original title.\"\"\"\n    assert c.get(\"/admin/reports\").text.count('id=\"report-') == 1\n    row = report_row(c, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] in row and \"Draft\" in row\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"method, path, data\", ROUTES)\ndef test_anonymous_visitor_is_redirected_from_every_report_route(\n        client, client_as, existing_report, method, path, data):\n    response = client.request(method, test_report_posts_without_csrf_token_are_rejected(\n        client_as, existing_report, method, path, data):\n    director = client_as(\"admin\")\n    director.get(\"/admin\")  # the session has a token; the POST just omits it\n    response = post_form(director, path.format(id=existing_report), data, with_csrf=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n    assert_unchanged(director)\n\n\ndef test_every_signed_in_user_sees_the_reports_link(client_as):\n    for role in (\"admin\", \"editor\"):\n        assert 'href=\"/admin/reports\"' in client_as(role).get(\"/admin\").text\n\n\ndef test_missing_report_is_404(client_as):\n    director = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert director.get(\"/admin/reports/999\").status_code == 404\n analyst.get(\"/admin/data-bites\").text\n    assert f\"<code>{REPORT['slug']}</code>\" in analyst.get(\"/admin/reports\").text\n\n\ndef test_a_draft_report_is_never_shown_publicly(client_as, client):\n    director, analyst = client_as(\"admin\"), client_as(\"editor\")\n    rid = published_report(director, analyst)\n    create_report(analyst, slug=\"cds-2027\", title=\"Unreleased CDS\")\n\n    home = client.get(\"/\").text\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] in home\n    assert \"Unreleased CDS\" not in home and \"cds-2027\" not in home\n\n    post_form(director, f\"/admin/reports/{rid}/unpublish\", {})\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] not in client.get(\"/\").text\n\n\n# Every Report route. {id} is filled with an existing draft Report's id.\nROUTES = [\n    (\"GET\", \"/admin/reports\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports\", {**REPORT, \"slug\": \"new-slug\"}),\n    (\"GET\", \"/admin/reports/{id}\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports/{id}\", {**REPORT, \"title\": \"Changed title\"}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports/{id}/publish\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports/{id}/unpublish\", {}),\n    (\"POST\", \"/admin/reports/{id}/delete\", {}),\n]\nPOST_ROUTES = [r for r in ROUTES if r[0] == \"POST\"]\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef existing_report(client_as):\n    return create_report(client_as(\"editor\"))\n\n\ndef assert_unchanged(c: TestClient):\n    \"\"\"The Report from `existing_report` is still the only one, still a draft,\n    with its original title.\"\"\"\n    assert c.get(\"/admin/reports\").text.count('id=\"report-') == 1\n    row = report_row(c, REPORT[\"slug\"])\n    assert REPORT[\"title\"] in row and \"Draft\" in row\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"method, path, data\", ROUTES)\ndef test_anonymous_visitor_is_redirected_from_every_report_route(\n        client, client_as, existing_report, method, path, data):\n    response = client.request(method, path.format(id=existing_report),\n                              data=data or None, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303, 307)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"].endswith(\"/login\")\n    assert_unchanged(client_as(\"admin\"))\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"method, path, data\", POST_ROUTES)\ndef test_report_posts_without_csrf_token_are_rejected(\n        client_as, existing_report, method, path, data):\n    director = client_as(\"admin\")\n    director.get(\"/admin\")  # the session has a token; the POST just omits it\n    response = post_form(director, path.format(id=existing_report), data, with_csrf=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n    assert_unchanged(director)\n\n\ndef test_every_signed_in_user_sees_the_reports_link(client_as):\n    for role in (\"admin\", \"editor\"):\n        assert 'href=\"/admin/reports\"' in client_as(role).get(\"/admin\").text\n\n\ndef test_missing_report_is_404(client_as):\n    director = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert director.get(\"/admin/reports/999\").status_code == 404\n    assert post_form(director, \"/admin/reports/999\", REPORT).status_code == 404\n    assert post_form(director, \"/admin/reports/999/publish\", {}).status_code == 404\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"75fe94aa-a41d-489c-9f4c-82b212b4fe16","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"62da7ed6-d7d3-4c7e-a6ef-a643980eed8f","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWbyYnSvsX29uXfvj1nU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01HzJojyb1navJNkzBWaHEBt","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/reports.py","content":"\"\"\"Reports: durable, cite-able Markdown documents (the Factbook, the CDS).\n\nAny Analyst or Director may create a Report (always as a draft) and edit a\ndraft one, whoever started it (shift continuity, ADR-003). Once a Report is\npublished only a Director may change it: `update` enforces that in the same\nstatement that writes, and the routes make publish, unpublish, and delete\nDirector only. Editing never changes the author or created_at.\n\nPublic-facing code reads Reports only through `list_published`, so a draft\ncannot leak through it.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom app.content import ContentError, duplicate_slug_error, validate\n\n_SELECT = (\"SELECT r.*, u.name AS author_name FROM reports r\"\n           \" JOIN users u ON u.id = r.author_id\")\n\n\nclass ReportLocked(ContentError):\n    \"\"\"A published Report, and the editor is not a Director.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef can_edit(report: sqlite3.Row, user: sqlite3.Row) -> bool:\n    return report[\"status\"] == \"draft\" or user[\"role\"] == \"admin\"\n\n\ndef create(title: str, slug: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Create a draft Report.\"\"\"\n    title, slug, body = validate(title, slug, body)\n    try:\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            cursor = conn.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO reports (title, slug, body, author_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (title, slug, body, author_id),\n            )\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        raise duplicate_slug_error(exc, \"reports\", \"Report\", slug) from None\n    return cursor.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update(report_id: int, title: str, slug: str, body: str, *,\n           editor: sqlite3.Row) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Edit title, slug, and body. The author, created_at, and status are kept.\n    False if there is no such Report; ReportLocked if it is published and the\n    editor is not a Director.\"\"\"\n    title, slug, body = validate(title, slug, body)\n    is_director = editor[\"role\"] == \"admin\"\n    try:\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            changed = conn.execute(\n                \"UPDATE reports SET title = ?, slug = ?, body = ?,\"\n                \" updated_at = datetime('now')\"\n                \" WHERE id = ? AND (status = 'draft' OR ?)\",\n                (title, slug, body, report_id, is_director),\n            ).rowcount == 1\n            if not changed and conn.execute(\n                    \"SELECT 1 FROM reports WHERE id = ?\", (report_id,)).fetchone():\n                raise ReportLocked(\"Only the Director can edit a published Report.\")\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        raise duplicate_slug_error(exc, \"reports\", \"Report\", slug) from None\n    return changed\n\n\ndef get(report_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} WHERE r.id = ?\", (report_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_all() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} ORDER BY r.updated_at DESC, r.id DESC\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef set_status(report_id: int, status: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Publish or unpublish (Director only; the routes check). False if there is\n    no such Report.\"\"\"\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE reports SET status = ?, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?\",\n            (status, report_id),\n        ).rowcount == 1\n\n\ndef delete(report_id: int) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Director only; the routes check. False if there is no such Report.\"\"\"\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM reports WHERE id = ?\", (report_id,)).rowcount == 1\n\n\ndef list_published() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"The public-facing query: published Reports only, by title.\"\"\"\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            f\"{_SELECT} WHERE r.status = 'published' ORDER BY r.title, r.id\"\n        ).fetchall()\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1484,"cache_read_input_tokens":79077,"output_tokens":3842,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":30},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1484,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3842,"cache_read_input_tokens":79077,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1484,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1484},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01HzJojyb1navJNkzBWaHEBt":{"file_path":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/reports.py","content":"\"\"\"Reports: durable, cite-able Markdown documents (the Factbook, the CDS).\n\nAny Analyst or Director may create a Report (always as a draft) and edit a\ndraft one, whoever started it (shift continuity, ADR-003). Editing never changes the author or created_at.\n\nPublic-facing code reads Reports only through `list_published`, so a draft\ncannot leak through it.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom app.content import ContentError, duplicate_slug_error, validate\n\n_SELECT = (\"SELECT r.*, u.name AS author_name FROM reports r\"\n           \" JOIN users u ON u.id = r.author_id\")\n\n\nclass ReportLocked(ContentError):\n    \"\"\"A published Report, and the editor is not a Director.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef can_edit(report: sqlite3.Row, user: sqlite3.Row) -> bool:\n    return report[\"status\"] == \"draft\" or user[\"role\"] == \"admin\"\n\n\ndef create(title: str, slug: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Create a draft Report.\"\"\"\n    title, slug, body = validate(title, slug, body)\n    try:\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            cursor = conn.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO reports (title, slug, body, author_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (title, slug, body, author_id),\n            )\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        raise duplicate_slug_error(exc, \"reports\", \"Report\", slug) from None\n    return cursor.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update(report_id: int, title: str, slug: str, body: str, *,\n           editor: sqlite3.Row) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Edit title, slug, and body. The author, created_at, and status are kept.\n    False if there is no such Report; ReportLocked if it is published and the\n    editor is not a Director.\"\"\"\n    title, slug, body = validate(title, slug, body)\n    is_director = editor[\"role\"] == \"admin\"\n    try:\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            changed = conn.execute(\n                \"UPDATE reports SET title = ?, slug = ?, body = ?,\"\n                \" updated_at = datetime('now')\"\n                \" WHERE id = ? AND (status = 'draft' OR ?)\",\n                (title, slug, body, report_id, is_director),\n            ).rowcount == 1\n            if not changed and conn.execute(\n                    \"SELECT 1 FROM reports WHERE id = ?\", (report_id,)).fetchone():\n                raise ReportLocked(\"Only the Director can edit a published Report.\")\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        raise duplicate_slug_error(exc, \"reports\", \"Report\", slug) from None\n    return changed\n\n\ndef get(report_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} WHERE r.id = ?\", (report_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_all() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} ORDER BY r.updated_at DESC, r.id DESC\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef set_status(report_id: int, status: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Publish or unpublish (Director only; the routes check). False if there is\n    no such Report.\"\"\"\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE reports SET status = ?, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?\",\n            (status, report_id),\n        ).rowcount == 1\n\n\ndef delete(report_id: int) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Director only; the routes check. False if there is no such Report.\"\"\"\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM reports WHERE id = ?\", (report_id,)).rowcount == 1\n\n\ndef list_published() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"The public-facing query: published Reports only, by title.\"\"\"\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            f\"{_SELECT} WHERE r.status = 'published' ORDER BY r.title, r.id\"\n        ).fetchall()\n"}},"apiBlockIndex":3,"requestId":"req_011CfWbyYMA36NhQaJuLr7Bw","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:implement","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"95953649-3353-4e47-b930-564d8c7a342f","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:11:34.118Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"high","perTurnEffort":"high","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"c3e265da-86b2-4ea5-a02e-dfe145db548e","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWbyYnSvsX29uXfvj1nU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CX4gziUo346HVYaEzR6BLN","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/routes/reports.py","content":"\"\"\"Reports in the admin console. Any signed-in user may list Reports, create a\ndraft, and edit a draft. Editing a published Report, and publishing,\nunpublishing, or deleting any Report, is Director only.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import reports\nfrom app.auth import current_user, require_csrf, require_director\nfrom app.content import STATUS_LABELS, ContentError\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/reports\", dependencies=[Depends(current_user)])\n\n\ndef _list_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, *, error: str | None = None,\n               form: dict | None = None, status_code: int = 200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/reports.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Reports\", \"home_path\": \"/admin\", \"user\": user,\n         \"reports\": reports.list_all(), \"status_labels\": STATUS_LABELS,\n         \"error\": error, \"form\": form or {}},\n        status_code=status_code,\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef list_reports(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return _list_page(request, user)\n\n\n@router.post(\"\", dependencies=[Depends(require_csrf)])\ndef create_report(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n                  slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\")):\n    try:\n        reports.create(title, slug, body, user[\"id\"])\n    except ContentError as exc:\n        return _list_page(request, user, error=str(exc), status_code=400,\n                          form={\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body})\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/reports\", status_code=303)\n\n\ndef _edit_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, report: sqlite3.Row, *,\n               error: str | None = None, form: dict | None = None,\n               status_code: int = 200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/report_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit Report\", \"home_path\": \"/admin\", \"user\": user,\n         \"report\": report, \"can_edit\": reports.can_edit(report, user),\n         \"status_labels\": STATUS_LABELS, \"error\": error, \"form\": form or report},\n        status_code=status_code,\n    )\n\n\ndef _get_or_404(report_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    report = reports.get(report_id)\n    if report is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n    return report\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{report_id}\")\ndef edit_form(request: Request, report_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return _edit_page(request, user, _get_or_404(report_id))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{report_id}\", dependencies=[Depends(require_csrf)])\ndef edit_report(request: Request, report_id: int, user=Depends(current_user),\n                title: str = Form(\"\"), slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\")):\n    report = _get_or_404(report_id)\n    try:\n        if not reports.update(report_id, title, slug, body, editor=user):\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n    except reports.ReportLocked as exc:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=str(exc)) from None\n    except ContentError as exc:\n        return _edit_page(request, user, report, error=str(exc), status_code=400,\n                          form={\"title\": title, > templates/admin/reports.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Reports</h1>\n  {% if user.role != \"admin\" %}\n  <p>Only the Director can publish a Report, or change one once it is published.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  {% if reports %}\n  <table>\n    <thead><tr><th>Title</th><th>Slug</th><th>Status</th><th>Author</th><th>Updated</th>{% if user.role == \"admin\" %}<th></th>{% endif %}</tr></thead>\n    <tbody>\n    {% for r in reports %}\n      <tr id=\"report-{{ r.id }}\">\n        <td><a href=\"/admin/reports/{{ r.id }}\">{{ r.title }}</a></td>\n        <td><code>{{ r.slug }}</code></td>\n        <td>{{ status_labels[r.status] }}</td>\n        <td>{{ r.author_name }}</td>\n        <td>{{ r.updated_at }}</td>\n        {% if user.role == \"admin\" %}\n        <td>\n          {% set action = \"unpublish\" if r.status == \"published\" else \"publish\" %}\n          <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/reports/{{ r.id }}/{{ action }}\">\n            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n            <button type=\"submit\">{{ action | capitalize }}</button>\n          </form>\n          <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/reports/{{ r.id }}/delete\">\n            <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n            <button type=\"submit\">Delete</button>\n          </form>\n        </td>\n        {% endif %}\n      </tr>\n    {% endfor %}\n    </tbody>\n  </table>\n  {% else %}\n  <p>No Reports yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n\n  <h2>New Report</h2>\n  {% set action = \"/admin/reports\" %}\n  {% set submit = \"Save draft\" %}\n  {% include \"admin/_content_fields.html\" %}\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/report_edit.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/reports\">All Reports</a></p>\n  <h1>{{ \"Edit Report\" if can_edit else report.title }}</h1>\n  <p>{{ status_labels[report.status] }}. Created {{ report.created_at }} by {{ report.author_name }}.\n     Last updated {{ report.updated_at }}.</p>\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  {% if can_edit %}\n  {% set action = \"/admin/reports/\" ~ report.id %}\n  {% set submit = \"Save changes\" %}\n  {% include \"admin/_content_fields.html\" %}\n  {% else %}\n  <p>Only the Director can edit, unpublish, or delete a published Report.</p>\n  <p>Slug: <code>{{ report.slug }}</code></p>\n  <pre>{{ report.body }}</pre>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\npython3 - <<'EOF'\nfrom pathlib import Path\np = {"parentUuid":"bf2e2850-8f88-448b-bbc8-6195c176be4b","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"04e24caf-e76d-4ac6-b3f7-74cb97b3f994","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/davelleat/.claude/plugins/cache/claude-plugins-official/mattpocock-skills/1.2.3/skills/engineering/code-review\n\nTwo-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:\n\n- **Standards**: does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?\n- **Spec**: does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / spec?\n\nBoth axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.\n\nThe issue tracker should have been provided to you. If `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md` is missing, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Pin the fixed point\n\nWhatever the user said is the fixed point (a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc.). If they didn't specify one, ask for it.\n\nCapture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.\n\nBefore going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here, not inside two parallel sub-agents.\n\n### 2. Identify the spec source\n\nLook for the originating spec, in this order:\n\n1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.), fetched via the workflow in `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\n2. A path the user passed as an argument.\n3. A spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.\n4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report \"no spec available\".\n\n### 3. Identify the standards sources\n\nAnything in the repo that documents how code should be written, such as `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md`.\n\nOn top of whatever the repo documents, the Standards axis always carries the **smell baseline** below: a fixed set of Fowler code smells (_Refactoring_, ch.3) that applies even when a repo documents nothing. Two rules bind it:\n\n- **The repo overrides.** A documented repo standard always wins; where it endorses something the baseline would flag, suppress the smell.\n- **Always a judgement call.** Each smell is a labelled heuristic (\"possible Feature Envy\"), never a hard violation. Like any standard here, skip anything tooling already enforces.\n\nEach smell reads *what it is* → *how to fix*; match it against the diff:\n\n- **Mysterious Name**: a function, variable, or type whose name doesn't reveal what it does or holds. → rename it; if no honest name comes, the design's murky.\n- **Duplicated Code**: the same logic shape appears in more than one hunk or file in the change. → extract the shared shape, call it from both.\n- **Feature Envy**: a method that reaches into another object's data more than its own. → move the method onto the data it envies.\n- **Data Clumps**: the same few fields or params keep travelling together (a type wanting to be born). → bundle them into one type, pass that.\n- **Primitive Obsession**: a primitive or string standing in for a domain concept that deserves its own type. → give the concept its own small type.\n- **Repeated Switches**: the same `switch`/`if`-cascade on the same type recurs across the change. → replace with polymorphism, or one map both sites share.\n- **Shotgun Surgery**: one logical change forces scattered edits across many files in the diff. → gather what changes together into one module.\n- **Divergent Change**: one file or module is edited for several unrelated reasons. → split so each module changes for one reason.\n- **Speculative Generality**: abstraction, parameters, or hooks added for needs the spec doesn't have. → delete it; inline back until a real need shows.\n- **Message Chains**: long `a.b().c().d()` navigation the caller shouldn't depend on. → hide the walk behind one method on the first object.\n- **Middle Man**: a class or function that mostly just delegates onward. → cut it, call the real target direct.\n- **Refused Bequest**: a subclass or implementer that ignores or overrides most of what it inherits. → drop the inheritance, use composition.\n\n### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel\n\n**Standards sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The full diff command and commit list.\n- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3, **plus the smell baseline from step 3** pasted in full (the sub-agent has no other access to it).\n- The brief: \"Report, per file/hunk where relevant, (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls: documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words.\"\n\n**Spec sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The diff command and commit list.\n- The path or fetched contents of the spec.\n- The brief: \"Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words.\"\n\nIf the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.\n\n### 5. Aggregate\n\nPresent the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings, because the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).\n\nEnd with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes: that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.\n\n## Why two axes\n\nA change can pass one axis and fail the other:\n\n- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing → **Standards pass, Spec fail.**\n- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions → **Spec pass, Standards fail.**\n\nReporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.\n\n\nARGUMENTS: Review uncommitted working-tree changes since HEAD (4b4d114) for ticket T04, issue #5 (spec #1). New files: app/content.py, app/reports.py, app/routes/reports.py, templates/admin/reports.html, templates/admin/report_edit.html, tests/test_t04_reports.py. Ignore notes/usage-ledger.csv and docs/transcripts."}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"7906b52c-2096-4a6f-89cd-85e3d6c048d5","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:13:09.888Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_01A7DxoLCdVBGtbi6GUrYc7N","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

Revisit if T04 Reports repeat it a third time.\" T04 extracted `app/content.py` for shared validation but `app/reports.py` / `app/routes/reports.py` still largely mirror the data_bites modules.
