Also compare with the existing T03 conventions in `app/data_bites.py` and `app/routes/data_bites.py` (as of HEAD: `git show HEAD:app/data_bites.py`).\n\nSmell baseline (Fowler, Refactoring ch.3) — always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides them:\n- Mysterious Name: a name that doesn't reveal what it does/holds → rename.\n- Duplicated Code: same logic shape in more than one hunk/file → extract shared shape.\n- Feature Envy: method reaching into another object's data more than its own → move it.\n- Data Clumps: same few fields/params travelling together → bundle into a type.\n- Primitive Obsession: primitive/string standing in for a domain concept → own small type.\n- Repeated Switches: same switch/if-cascade on same type recurs → polymorphism or shared map.\n- Shotgun Surgery: one logical change forces scattered edits across many files → gather into one module.\n- Divergent Change: one module edited for several unrelated reasons → split.\n- Speculative Generality: abstraction/params/hooks for needs the spec doesn't have → delete/inline.\n- Message Chains: long a.b().c().d() navigation → hide behind one method.\n- Middle Man: class/function that mostly delegates → cut it.\n- Refused Bequest: subclass ignoring most of what it inherits → composition.\n\nContext: the T03 review had noted \"Possible Data Clumps (title, slug, body) and page-helper shape repeated from _users_page — Kept: matches T02 convention. Revisit if T04 Reports repeat it a third time.\" T04 extracted `app/content.py` for shared validation but `app/reports.py` / `app/routes/reports.py` still largely mirror the data_bites modules. Judge whether that remaining duplication is worth flagging.\n\nBrief: Report, per file/hunk where relevant, (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls: documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words.","outputFile":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-davelleat-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820/tasks/a734bb0e132ca14e6.output","canReadOutputFile":true},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3cdb0292-68b1-49f3-8cd2-5e1082b53536","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"type":"queue-operation","operation":"enqueue","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:14:03.163Z","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","content":"<task-notification>\n<task-id>a734bb0e132ca14e6</task-id>\n<tool-use-id>toolu_01DupjKEgZd66NX2wX4qBBuG</tool-use-id>\n<output-file>/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-davelleat-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820/tasks/a734bb0e132ca14e6.output</output-file>\n<status>completed</status>\n<summary>Agent \"Standards review of T04 diff\" finished</summary>\n<note>A task-notification fires each time this agent stops with no live background children of its own. The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>I found no hard violations of the CLAUDE.md hard constraints or SECURITY-CHECKLIST.md. The main issues are two wrong ADR citations and the leftover duplication, which I think is now worth flagging.\n\n**Documented standards**\n\n- **CSRF** (CLAUDE.md, checklist #3): met. All four new POST routes depend on `require_csrf`, and `reports.html` puts the token in both inline forms.\n- **Access control in code** (checklist #10): met. Publish, unpublish and delete use `require_director`, and the published-Report lock is inside the SQL `WHERE` clause, not just hidden in the template.\n- **Markdown sanitizing, drafts, relative paths**: met. The read-only body is shown escaped in `&lt;pre&gt;{{ report.body }}&lt;/pre&gt;`. Public code reads Reports only through `list_published`. `public/home.html` adds no absolute paths.\n- **Wrong ADR citation (documentation accuracy; judgement, but a clear error)**: `app/content.py` says \"a Data Bite and a Report may share one (ADR-003's export-path namespacing)\", and `app/db.py` says `-- unique among Reports only (ADR-003)`. ADR-003 covers publish rights only, and no ADR mentions slugs. Either record the decision in an ADR or remove the citations.\n- **CONTEXT.md vocabulary (judgement)**: `update(..., *, editor: sqlite3.Row)` and \"the editor is not a Director\" use \"Editor\", which the glossary lists as a word to avoid for Analyst. Here it means \"whoever is editing\", but `actor` or `user` would avoid the clash.\n\n**Baseline smells (all judgement calls)**\n\n- **Duplicated Code, flagged**: This is the third copy that the T03 review said to revisit. `app/reports.py`'s `create`, `get`, `list_all`, `set_status` and `delete` match `data_bites.py` line for line, apart from the table name and noun. The routes' `_list_page`, `_edit_page`, `_get_or_404` and `_set_status` are also copies. Only `update`/`can_edit` and the `require_director` dependencies actually differ. I recommend moving the shared CRUD into `content.py`, parametrized by table, and keeping the permission rules in each module. With only two content types, the other acceptable option is to keep the copies and write the decision down, as T03 did.\n- **Data Clumps / Primitive Obsession**: `form={\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body}` now appears four times across the two route files. `duplicate_slug_error(exc, \"reports\", \"Report\", slug)` passes the table and noun as a pair of loose strings. The same small content-type object (table, noun) would fix both, and it fits the extraction above.\n- **Repeated Switches / Primitive Obsession**: `user[\"role\"] == \"admin\"` now appears in `reports.can_edit`, `reports.update` and `auth.require_director`, plus two template checks. An `auth.is_director(user)` helper would keep it in one place.\n- **Duplicated business rule**: \"draft, or the user is the Director\" is written twice, in `can_edit` (Python) and in the `UPDATE ... WHERE status = 'draft' OR ?` clause. It's defensible, since the SQL is the real guard, but add a comment linking the two.\n- **Duplicated Code (template)**: `public/home.html` copies the whole `&lt;ul&gt;{% for item in ... %}` loop for Reports. A macro would remove it.\n- **Mysterious Name**: in `main.py`, `items` (the Data Bites) sits next to `report_items`, which is asymmetric. `data_bite_items` would read better. `content.validate` is generic, though its module qualifier helps.\n- **Minor**: `routes/data_bites.py` now imports `app.content` before `app.auth`, while `routes/reports.py` does the reverse.</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>53189</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>5</tool_uses><duration_ms>38504</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"}

{"parentUuid":"b3390e15-44a5-40fc-9008-86b2842abb9b","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"e26a891c-a130-4482-b119-a352e5b2bc0a","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"<task-notification>\n<task-id>a734bb0e132ca14e6</task-id>\n<tool-use-id>toolu_01DupjKEgZd66NX2wX4qBBuG</tool-use-id>\n<output-file>/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-davelleat-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820/tasks/a734bb0e132ca14e6.output</output-file>\n<status>completed</status>\n<summary>Agent \"Standards review of T04 diff\" finished</summary>\n<note>A task-notification fires each time this agent stops with no live background children of its own. The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>I found no hard violations of the CLAUDE.md hard constraints or SECURITY-CHECKLIST.md. The main issues are two wrong ADR citations and the leftover duplication, which I think is now worth flagging.\n\n**Documented standards**\n\n- **CSRF** (CLAUDE.md, checklist #3): met. All four new POST routes depend on `require_csrf`, and `reports.html` puts the token in both inline forms.\n- **Access control in code** (checklist #10): met. Publish, unpublish and delete use `require_director`, and the published-Report lock is inside the SQL `WHERE` clause, not just hidden in the template.\n- **Markdown sanitizing, drafts, relative paths**: met. The read-only body is shown escaped in `&lt;pre&gt;{{ report.body }}&lt;/pre&gt;`. Public code reads Reports only through `list_published`. `public/home.html` adds no absolute paths.\n- **Wrong ADR citation (documentation accuracy; judgement, but a clear error)**: `app/content.py` says \"a Data Bite and a Report may share one (ADR-003's export-path namespacing)\", and `app/db.py` says `-- unique among Reports only (ADR-003)`. ADR-003 covers publish rights only, and no ADR mentions slugs. Either record the decision in an ADR or remove the citations.\n- **CONTEXT.md vocabulary (judgement)**: `update(..., *, editor: sqlite3.Row)` and \"the editor is not a Director\" use \"Editor\", which the glossary lists as a word to avoid for Analyst. Here it means \"whoever is editing\", but `actor` or `user` would avoid the clash.\n\n**Baseline smells (all judgement calls)**\n\n- **Duplicated Code, flagged**: This is the third copy that the T03 review said to revisit. `app/reports.py`'s `create`, `get`, `list_all`, `set_status` and `delete` match `data_bites.py` line for line, apart from the table name and noun. The routes' `_list_page`, `_edit_page`, `_get_or_404` and `_set_status` are also copies. Only `update`/`can_edit` and the `require_director` dependencies actually differ. I recommend moving the shared CRUD into `content.py`, parametrized by table, and keeping the permission rules in each module. With only two content types, the other acceptable option is to keep the copies and write the decision down, as T03 did.\n- **Data Clumps / Primitive Obsession**: `form={\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body}` now appears four times across the two route files. `duplicate_slug_error(exc, \"reports\", \"Report\", slug)` passes the table and noun as a pair of loose strings. The same small content-type object (table, noun) would fix both, and it fits the extraction above.\n- **Repeated Switches / Primitive Obsession**: `user[\"role\"] == \"admin\"` now appears in `reports.can_edit`, `reports.update` and `auth.require_director`, plus two template checks. An `auth.is_director(user)` helper would keep it in one place.\n- **Duplicated business rule**: \"draft, or the user is the Director\" is written twice, in `can_edit` (Python) and in the `UPDATE ... WHERE status = 'draft' OR ?` clause. It's defensible, since the SQL is the real guard, but add a comment linking the two.\n- **Duplicated Code (template)**: `public/home.html` copies the whole `&lt;ul&gt;{% for item in ... %}` loop for Reports. A macro would remove it.\n- **Mysterious Name**: in `main.py`, `items` (the Data Bites) sits next to `report_items`, which is asymmetric. `data_bite_items` would read better. `content.validate` is generic, though its module qualifier helps.\n- **Minor**: `routes/data_bites.py` now imports `app.content` before `app.auth`, while `routes/reports.py` does the reverse.</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>53189</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>5</tool_uses><duration_ms>38504</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"},"uuid":"8d015ce5-b2bf-45b0-9bc0-0bdc10e7a4e1","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:14:03.180Z","permissionMode":"bypassPermissions","origin":{"kind":"task-notification","producer":"session-task"},"promptSource":"system","turnOrigin":"task_notification","turnPosition":{"promptIndex":1,"turnIndex":2},"queueSkipAttachments":true,"userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>The T04 diff meets every acceptance criterion in #5, and all 49 tests pass (`tests/test_t04_reports.py`, `tests/test_t03_data_bites.py`). I found no missing features. The gaps are one test that's too thin, some small changes nobody asked for, and one ordering problem.\n\n**(a) Missing or partial**\n\n1. **The shift-continuity test only checks the author.** The spec says: *\"the original author and `created_at` are preserved, only `updated_at` changes.\"* `test_any_analyst_edits_any_draft_report_and_the_author_is_kept` checks the author only. The code does keep `created_at` and bumps `updated_at`, but no Report test proves it. T03 had a `backdate()` test that does this for Data Bites; Reports need the same. That weakens the AC \"Tests cover all of the above\".\n2. **The draft-leak test only checks `/`.** The AC is *\"A draft Report is never returned by any public-facing query.\"* For T04, `/` is the only public page, so this is fine for now. The real guarantee is that public code only calls `list_published()`. This needs checking again in T07 and T08.\n\n**(b) Not asked for (all small, all reasonable)**\n\n1. The shared title/slug/body checks moved into a new `app/content.py`. `DataBiteError` became `ContentError`, `_data_bite_fields.html` was renamed `_content_fields.html`, and `second_analyst` moved into `conftest.py`. This is a refactor of T03 code inside T04. No old names are left anywhere.\n2. Analysts get a read-only view of a published Report in `report_edit.html`: slug plus raw body in `&lt;pre&gt;`. The spec only says they can't edit it; it doesn't describe this page.\n3. The public home now has a \"Reports\" heading above the list, while Data Bites still have no heading. That's a small layout choice ahead of T07.\n\n**(c) Implemented, but looks wrong**\n\n1. **Content checks run before the permission check when editing.** `reports.update()` calls `validate()` before the locked `UPDATE`. So if an Analyst sends an invalid edit (such as an empty body or bad slug) to a *published* Report, they get **400** with an error message, not 403. Nothing changes, so the lock holds. But the AC says *\"Once published, Analyst gets 403/redirect attempting to edit\"*, and the spec says *\"Enforced server-side on every route.\"* The fix is to check `can_edit` (or the locked state) before `validate`, and add a test with an invalid body.\n2. **Minor: some Director-only buttons show only on the list page.** The spec says *\"Director can publish, edit, unpublish, and delete a Report in any state.\"* Publish, unpublish and delete are only on `/admin/reports`, not on the edit page. They all work, so this is a UX gap rather than a spec failure.\n\nEverything else holds up:\n- **Permissions:** publish, unpublish and delete are Director-only through `require_director`. The published-Report lock is enforced inside the same `UPDATE` statement that writes, so there's no race.\n- **Slugs:** each type has its own `UNIQUE` column, so a duplicate within Reports is rejected but a Data Bite can share a Report's slug. Both cases are tested.\n- **CSRF:** every POST is covered, and each has a test that strips the token.\n- **Anonymous visitors:** they're redirected to login on every Report route, and that's tested.\n\nFiles: `/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/reports.py`, `/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/routes/reports.py`, `/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t04_reports.py`</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>59124</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>6</tool_uses><duration_ms>43617</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"}

The gaps are one test that's too thin, some small changes nobody asked for, and one ordering problem.\n\n**(a) Missing or partial**\n\n1. **The shift-continuity test only checks the author.** The spec says: *\"the original author and `created_at` are preserved, only `updated_at` changes.\"* `test_any_analyst_edits_any_draft_report_and_the_author_is_kept` checks the author only. The code does keep `created_at` and bumps `updated_at`, but no Report test proves it. T03 had a `backdate()` test that does this for Data Bites; Reports need the same. That weakens the AC \"Tests cover all of the above\".\n2. **The draft-leak test only checks `/`.** The AC is *\"A draft Report is never returned by any public-facing query.\"* For T04, `/` is the only public page, so this is fine for now. The real guarantee is that public code only calls `list_published()`. This needs checking again in T07 and T08.\n\n**(b) Not asked for (all small, all reasonable)**\n\n1. The shared title/slug/body checks moved into a new `app/content.py`. `DataBiteError` became `ContentError`, `_data_bite_fields.html` was renamed `_content_fields.html`, and `second_analyst` moved into `conftest.py`. This is a refactor of T03 code inside T04. No old names are left anywhere.\n2. Analysts get a read-only view of a published Report in `report_edit.html`: slug plus raw body in `&lt;pre&gt;`. The spec only says they can't edit it; it doesn't describe this page.\n3. The public home now has a \"Reports\" heading above the list, while Data Bites still have no heading. That's a small layout choice ahead of T07.\n\n**(c) Implemented, but looks wrong**\n\n1. **Content checks run before the permission check when editing.** `reports.update()` calls `validate()` before the locked `UPDATE`. So if an Analyst sends an invalid edit (such as an empty body or bad slug) to a *published* Report, they get **400** with an error message, not 403. Nothing changes, so the lock holds. But the AC says *\"Once published, Analyst gets 403/redirect attempting to edit\"*, and the spec says *\"Enforced server-side on every route.\"* The fix is to check `can_edit` (or the locked state) before `validate`, and add a test with an invalid body.\n2. **Minor: some Director-only buttons show only on the list page.** The spec says *\"Director can publish, edit, unpublish, and delete a Report in any state.\"* Publish, unpublish and delete are only on `/admin/reports`, not on the edit page. They all work, so this is a UX gap rather than a spec failure.\n\nEverything else holds up:\n- **Permissions:** publish, unpublish and delete are Director-only through `require_director`. The published-Report lock is enforced inside the same `UPDATE` statement that writes, so there's no race.\n- **Slugs:** each type has its own `UNIQUE` column, so a duplicate within Reports is rejected but a Data Bite can share a Report's slug. Both cases are tested.\n- **CSRF:** every POST is covered, and each has a test that strips the token.\n- **Anonymous visitors:** they're redirected to login on every Report route, and that's tested.\n\nFiles: `/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/reports.py`, `/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/routes/reports.py`, `/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t04_reports.py`</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>59124</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>6</tool_uses><duration_ms>43617</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"},"uuid":"bc38548d-5243-4fc8-aa36-6b34874056ce","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:14:27.538Z","permissionMode":"bypassPermissions","origin":{"kind":"task-notification","producer":"session-task"},"promptSource":"system","turnOrigin":"task_notification","turnPosition":{"promptIndex":1,"turnIndex":3},"queueSkipAttachments":true,"userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

Reports keep their own lockdown rule."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3758,"cache_read_input_tokens":101056,"output_tokens":4319,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":955},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3758,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":4,"output_tokens":4319,"cache_read_input_tokens":101056,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3758,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3758},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfWcDWKWqYVE5iYbZZLkG","type":"assistant","uuid":"dfdc27fa-f1c0-45ab-bbcb-32059baaeaa0","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:14:40.239Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"high","perTurnEffort":"high","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"dfdc27fa-f1c0-45ab-bbcb-32059baaeaa0","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWcDX3Qs2AhERefrrisJ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01YGdjq6kHUYw1HDiqzagStL","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/content.py","content":"\"\"\"What Data Bites and Reports share: the title/slug/Markdown-body fields, their\nvalidation, the draft/published status, and the table-level reads and writes.\n\nEach content type keeps its own table, so a slug is unique within a type but a\nData Bite and a Report may share one (spec #1, \"Publish gate & export paths\":\nexports are namespaced by type). Who may do what is decided by each type's own\nmodule and routes, not here.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\nfrom app import db\n\nSTATUS_LABELS = {\"draft\": \"Draft\", \"published\": \"Published\"}\n_SLUG_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*\")\n\n\nclass ContentError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"Content broke a rule; the message is safe to show.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass DuplicateSlug(ContentError):\n    pass\n\n\ndef validate(title: str, slug: str, body: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:\n    title, slug = title.strip(), slug.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise ContentError(\"Enter a title.\")\n    if not _SLUG_PATTERN.fullmatch(slug):\n        raise ContentError(\"Slugs use lowercase letters, digits, and single \"\n                           \"hyphens, e.g. fall-enrollment.\")\n    if not body.strip():\n        raise ContentError(\"Enter a body.\")\n    return title, slug, body\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass ContentTable:\n    \"\"\"One content type's table. `table` is a fixed identifier from this\n    codebase, never user input.\"\"\"\n    table: str  # e.g. \"reports\"\n    noun: str   # e.g. \"Report\", for messages\n\n    def _select(self, where: str, order: str, params: tuple = ()) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            return conn.execute(\n                f\"SELECT c.*, u.name AS author_name FROM {self.table} c\"\n                f\" JOIN users u ON u.id = c.author_id WHERE {where} ORDER BY {order}\",\n                params,\n            ).fetchall()\n\n    def _duplicate_slug_error(self, exc: sqlite3.IntegrityError, slug: str) -> DuplicateSlug:\n        \"\"\"Translate the slug's UNIQUE violation; re-raise any other constraint.\"\"\"\n        if f\"{self.table}.slug\" not in str(exc):\n            raise exc\n        return DuplicateSlug(f\"Another {self.noun} already uses the slug {slug}.\")\n\n    def create(self, title: str, slug: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Create a draft.\"\"\"\n        title, slug, body = validate(title, slug, body)\n        try:\n            with db.connect() as conn:\n                cursor = conn.execute(\n                    f\"INSERT INTO {self.table} (title, slug, body, author_id)\"\n                    \" VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                    (title, slug, body, author_id),\n                )\n        except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n            raise self._duplicate_slug_error(exc, slug) from None\n        return cursor.lastrowid\n\n    def update(self, item_id: int, title: str, slug: str, body: str, *,\n               drafts_only: bool = False) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Edit title, slug, and body. The author, created_at, and status are\n        kept. With drafts_only, a published item is left alone. False if\n        nothing was changed.\"\"\"\n        title, slug, body = validate(title, slug, body)\n        try:\n            with db.connect() as conn:\n                return conn.execute(\n                    f\"UPDATE {self.table} SET title = ?, slug = ?, body = ?,\"\n                    \" updated_at = datetime('now')\"\n                    \" WHERE id = ? AND (status = 'draft' OR NOT ?)\",\n                    (title, slug, body, item_id, drafts_only),\n                ).rowcount == 1\n        except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n            raise self._duplicate_slug_error(exc, slug) from None\n\n    def get(self, item_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n        rows = self._select(\"c.id = ?\", \"c.id\", (item_id,))\n        return rows[0] if rows else None\n\n    def list_all(self) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n        return self._select(\"1\", \"c.updated_at DESC, c.id DESC\")\n\n    def list_published(self, order: str) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n        return self._select(\"c.status = 'published'\", order)\n\n    def set_status(self, item_id: int, status: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Publish or unpublish. False if there is no such item.\"\"\"\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            return conn.execute(\n                f\"UPDATE {self.table} SET status = ?, updated_at = datetime('now')\"\n                \" WHERE id = ?\",\n                (status, item_id),\n            ).rowcount == 1\n\n    def delete(self, item_id: int) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"False if there is no such item.\"\"\"\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            return conn.execute(\n                f\"DELETE FROM {self.table} WHERE id = ?\", (item_id,)).rowcount == 1\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3758,"cache_read_input_tokens":101056,"output_tokens":4319,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":955},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3758,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":4,"output_tokens":4319,"cache_read_input_tokens":101056,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3758,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3758},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01YGdjq6kHUYw1HDiqzagStL":{"file_path":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/content.py","content":"\"\"\"What Data Bites and Reports share: the title/slug/Markdown-body fields, their\nvalidation, the draft/published status, and the table-level reads and writes.\n\nEach content type keeps its own table, so a slug is unique within a type but a\nData Bite and a Report may share one (spec #1, \"Publish gate & export paths\":\nexports are namespaced by type). Who may do what is decided by each type's own\nmodule and routes, not here.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\nfrom app import db\n\nSTATUS_LABELS = {\"draft\": \"Draft\", \"published\": \"Published\"}\n_SLUG_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*\")\n\n\nclass ContentError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"Content broke a rule; the message is safe to show.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass DuplicateSlug(ContentError):\n    pass\n\n\ndef validate(title: str, slug: str, body: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:\n    title, slug = title.strip(), slug.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise ContentError(\"Enter a title.\")\n    if not _SLUG_PATTERN.fullmatch(slug):\n        raise ContentError(\"Slugs use lowercase letters, digits, and single \"\n                           \"hyphens, e.g. fall-enrollment.\")\n    if not body.strip():\n        raise ContentError(\"Enter a body.\")\n    return title, slug, body\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass ContentTable:\n    \"\"\"One content type's table. `table` is a fixed identifier from this\n    codebase, never user input.\"\"\"\n    table: str  # e.g. \"reports\"\n    noun: str   # e.g. \"Report\", for messages\n\n    def _select(self, where: str, order: str, params: tuple = ()) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            return conn.execute(\n                f\"SELECT c.*, u.name AS author_name FROM {self.table} c\"\n                f\" JOIN users u ON u.id = c.author_id WHERE {where} ORDER BY {order}\",\n                params,\n            ).fetchall()\n\n    def _duplicate_slug_error(self, exc: sqlite3.IntegrityError, slug: str) -> DuplicateSlug:\n        \"\"\"Translate the slug's UNIQUE violation; re-raise any other constraint.\"\"\"\n        if f\"{self.table}.slug\" not in str(exc):\n            raise exc\n        return DuplicateSlug(f\"Another {self.noun} already uses the slug {slug}.\")\n\n    def create(self, title: str, slug: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Create a draft.\"\"\"\n        title, slug, body = validate(title, slug, body)\n        try:\n            with db.connect() as conn:\n                cursor = conn.execute(\n                    f\"INSERT INTO {self.table} (title, slug, body, author_id)\"\n                    \" VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                    (title, slug, body, author_id),\n                )\n        except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n            raise self._duplicate_slug_error(exc, slug) from None\n        return cursor.lastrowid\n\n    def update(self, item_id: int, title: str, slug: str, body: str, *,\n               drafts_only: bool = False) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Edit title, slug, and body. The author, created_at, and status are\n        kept. With drafts_only, a published item is left alone. False if\n        nothing was changed.\"\"\"\n        title, slug, body = validate(title, slug, body)\n        try:\n            with db.connect() as conn:\n                return conn.execute(\n                    f\"UPDATE {self.table} SET title = ?, slug = ?, body = ?,\"\n                    \" updated_at = datetime('now')\"\n                    \" WHERE id = ? AND (status = 'draft' OR NOT ?)\",\n                    (title, slug, body, item_id, drafts_only),\n                ).rowcount == 1\n        except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n            raise self._duplicate_slug_error(exc, slug) from None\n\n    def get(self, item_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n        rows = self._select(\"c.id = ?\", \"c.id\", (item_id,))\n        return rows[0] if rows else None\n\n    def list_all(self) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n        return self._select(\"1\", \"c.updated_at DESC, c.id DESC\")\n\n    def list_published(self, order: str) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n        return self._select(\"c.status = 'published'\", order)\n\n    def set_status(self, item_id: int, status: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Publish or unpublish. False if there is no such item.\"\"\"\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            return conn.execute(\n                f\"UPDATE {self.table} SET status = ?, updated_at = datetime('now')\"\n                \" WHERE id = ?\",\n                (status, item_id),\n            ).rowcount == 1\n\n    def delete(self, item_id: int) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"False if there is no such item.\"\"\"\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            return conn.execute(\n                f\"DELETE FROM {self.table} WHERE id = ?\", (item_id,)).rowcount == 1\n"}},"apiBlockIndex":2,"requestId":"req_011CfWcDWKWqYVE5iYbZZLkG","type":"assistant","uuid":"8e8c49ad-e772-49bd-841c-74d868558cab","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:14:52.659Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"high","perTurnEffort":"high","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

the message is safe to show.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass DuplicateSlug(ContentError):\n    pass\n\n\ndef validate(title: str, slug: str, body: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:\n    title, slug = title.strip(), slug.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise ContentError(\"Enter a title.\")\n    if not _SLUG_PATTERN.fullmatch(slug):\n        raise ContentError(\"Slugs use lowercase letters, digits, and single \"\n                           \"hyphens, e.g. fall-enrollment.\")\n    if not body.strip():\n        raise ContentError(\"Enter a body.\")\n    return title, slug, body\n\n\ndef duplicate_slug_error(exc: sqlite3.IntegrityError, table: str, noun: str,\n                         slug: str) -> DuplicateSlug:\n    \"\"\"Translate `table`'s slug UNIQUE violation; {"parentUuid":"f507c2b1-4605-451b-8600-9b4b1104fa25","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWcDX3Qs2AhERefrrisJ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Efm2stcvuLCUbPqqBHi8de","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/data_bites.py","content":"\"\"\"Data Bites: short, dated Markdown items.\n\nAny Analyst or Director may create, edit, publish, and unpublish any Data Bite\n(shift continuity, ADR-003); only a Director may delete one, which the routes\nenforce. Editing never changes the author or created_at.\n\nPublic-facing code reads Data Bites only through `list_published`, so a draft\ncannot leak through it.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app.content import ContentTable\n\n_TABLE = ContentTable(\"data_bites\", \"Data Bite\")\n\ncreate = _TABLE.create\nget = _TABLE.get\nlist_all = _TABLE.list_all\nset_status = _TABLE.set_status\ndelete = _TABLE.delete\n\n\ndef update(bite_id: int, title: str, slug: str, body: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Edit title, slug, and body. False if there is no such Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    return _TABLE.update(bite_id, title, slug, body)\n\n\ndef list_published() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"The public-facing query: published Data Bites only, newest first.\"\"\"\n    return _TABLE.list_published(\"c.created_at DESC, c.id DESC\")\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3758,"cache_read_input_tokens":101056,"output_tokens":4319,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":955},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3758,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":4,"output_tokens":4319,"cache_read_input_tokens":101056,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3758,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3758},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01Efm2stcvuLCUbPqqBHi8de":{"file_path":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/data_bites.py","content":"\"\"\"Data Bites: short, dated Markdown items.\n\nAny Analyst or Director may create, edit, publish, and unpublish any Data Bite\n(shift continuity, ADR-003); only a Director may delete one, which the routes\nenforce. Editing never changes the author or created_at.\n\nPublic-facing code reads Data Bites only through `list_published`, so a draft\ncannot leak through it.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app.content import ContentTable\n\n_TABLE = ContentTable(\"data_bites\", \"Data Bite\")\n\ncreate = _TABLE.create\nget = _TABLE.get\nlist_all = _TABLE.list_all\nset_status = _TABLE.set_status\ndelete = _TABLE.delete\n\n\ndef update(bite_id: int, title: str, slug: str, body: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Edit title, slug, and body. False if there is no such Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    return _TABLE.update(bite_id, title, slug, body)\n\n\ndef list_published() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"The public-facing query: published Data Bites only, newest first.\"\"\"\n    return _TABLE.list_published(\"c.created_at DESC, c.id DESC\")\n"}},"apiBlockIndex":3,"requestId":"req_011CfWcDWKWqYVE5iYbZZLkG","type":"assistant","uuid":"55d1f5e1-6057-4eef-a602-ebe7ac418aaa","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:14:55.804Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"high","perTurnEffort":"high","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

Editing never changes the author or created_at.\n\nPublic-facing code reads Data Bites only through `list_published`, so a draft\ncannot leak through it.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app.content import ContentTable\n\n_TABLE = ContentTable(\"data_bites\", \"Data Bite\")\n\ncreate = _TABLE.create\nget = _TABLE.get\nlist_all = _TABLE.list_all\nset_status = _TABLE.set_status\ndelete = _TABLE.delete\n\n\ndef update(bite_id: int, title: str, slug: str, body: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Edit title, slug, and body. False if there is no such Data Bite.\"\"\"","-    with db.connect() as conn:","-        return conn.execute(","-            \"UPDATE data_bites SET status = ?, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?\",","-            (status, bite_id),","-        ).rowcount == 1","-","-","-def delete(bite_id: int) -> bool:","-    \"\"\"False if there is no such Data Bite.\"\"\"","-    with db.connect() as conn:","-        return conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM data_bites WHERE id = ?\", (bite_id,)).rowcount == 1","-","-"," def list_published() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:","     \"\"\"The public-facing query: published Data Bites only, newest first.\"\"\"","-    with db.connect() as conn:","-        return conn.execute(","-            f\"{_SELECT} WHERE d.status = 'published' ORDER BY d.created_at DESC, d.id DESC\"","-        ).fetchall()","+    return _TABLE.list_published(\"c.created_at DESC, c.id DESC\")"]}],"originalFile":"\"\"\"Data Bites: short, dated Markdown items.\n\nAny Analyst or Director may create, edit, publish, and unpublish any Data Bite\n(shift continuity, ADR-003); only a Director may delete one, which the routes\nenforce. Editing never changes the author or created_at.\n\nPublic-facing code reads Data Bites only through `list_published`, so a draft\ncannot leak through it.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom app.content import duplicate_slug_error, validate\n\n_SELECT = (\"SELECT d.*, u.name AS author_name FROM data_bites d\"\n           \" JOIN users u ON u.id = d.author_id\")\n\n\ndef create(title: str, slug: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Create a draft Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    title, slug, body = validate(title, slug, body)\n    try:\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            cursor = conn.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO data_bites (title, slug, body, author_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (title, slug, body, author_id),\n            )\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        raise duplicate_slug_error(exc, \"data_bites\", \"Data Bite\", slug) from None\n    return cursor.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update(bite_id: int, title: str, slug: str, body: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Edit title, slug, and body. The author, created_at, and status are kept.\n    False if there is no such Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    title, slug, body = validate(title, slug, body)\n    try:\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            return conn.execute(\n                \"UPDATE data_bites SET title = ?, slug = ?, body = ?,\"\n                \" updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?\",\n                (title, slug, body, bite_id),\n            ).rowcount == 1\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        raise duplicate_slug_error(exc, \"data_bites\", \"Data Bite\", slug) from None\n\n\ndef get(bite_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} WHERE d.id = ?\", (bite_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_all() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} ORDER BY d.updated_at DESC, d.id DESC\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef set_status(bite_id: int, status: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Publish or unpublish. False if there is no such Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE data_bites SET status = ?, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?\",\n            (status, bite_id),\n        ).rowcount == 1\n\n\ndef delete(bite_id: int) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"False if there is no such Data Bite.\"\"\"\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM data_bites WHERE id = ?\", (bite_id,)).rowcount == 1\n\n\ndef list_published() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"The public-facing query: published Data Bites only, newest first.\"\"\"\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            f\"{_SELECT} WHERE d.status = 'published' ORDER BY d.created_at DESC, d.id DESC\"\n        ).fetchall()\n","userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"55d1f5e1-6057-4eef-a602-ebe7ac418aaa","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"7b447cdf-7872-4e22-8075-f11f1ba947d0","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWcDX3Qs2AhERefrrisJ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011N6G8gJhGXxFBzvEw8XoWv","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/reports.py","content":"\"\"\"Reports: durable, cite-able Markdown documents (the Factbook, the CDS).\n\nAny Analyst or Director may create a Report (always as a draft) and edit a\ndraft one, whoever started it (shift continuity, ADR-003). Once a Report is\npublished only a Director may change it: `update` enforces that in the same\nstatement that writes, and the routes make publish, unpublish, and delete\nDirector only. Editing never changes the author or created_at.\n\nPublic-facing code reads Reports only through `list_published`, so a draft\ncannot leak through it.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app.content import ContentError, ContentTable\nfrom app.users import is_director\n\n_TABLE = ContentTable(\"reports\", \"Report\")\n\ncreate = _TABLE.create\nget = _TABLE.get\nlist_all = _TABLE.list_all\nset_status = _TABLE.set_status\ndelete = _TABLE.delete\n\n\nclass ReportLocked(ContentError):\n    \"\"\"A published Report, and the user is not a Director.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef can_edit(report: sqlite3.Row, user: sqlite3.Row) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"The lockdown rule. `update` applies the same rule in its UPDATE.\"\"\"\n    return report[\"status\"] == \"draft\" or is_director(user)\n\n\ndef update(report_id: int, title: str, slug: str, body: str, *,\n           user: sqlite3.Row) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Edit title, slug, and body on behalf of `user`. False if there is no\n    such Report; ReportLocked if it is published and `user` is not a Director.\"\"\"\n    if _TABLE.update(report_id, title, slug, body, drafts_only=not is_director(user)):\n        return True\n    if get(report_id) is not None:\n        raise ReportLocked(\"Only the Director can edit a published Report.\")\n    return False\n\n\ndef list_published() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"The public-facing query: published Reports only, by title.\"\"\"\n    return _TABLE.list_published(\"c.title, c.id\")\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3758,"cache_read_input_tokens":101056,"output_tokens":4319,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":955},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3758,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":4,"output_tokens":4319,"cache_read_input_tokens":101056,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3758,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3758},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_011N6G8gJhGXxFBzvEw8XoWv":{"file_path":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/reports.py","content":"\"\"\"Reports: durable, cite-able Markdown documents (the Factbook, the CDS).\n\nAny Analyst or Director may create a Report (always as a draft) and edit a\ndraft one, whoever started it (shift continuity, ADR-003). Once a Report is\npublished only a Director may change it: `update` enforces that in the same\nstatement that writes, and the routes make publish, unpublish, and delete\nDirector only. Editing never changes the author or created_at.\n\nPublic-facing code reads Reports only through `list_published`, so a draft\ncannot leak through it.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app.content import ContentError, ContentTable\nfrom app.users import is_director\n\n_TABLE = ContentTable(\"reports\", \"Report\")\n\ncreate = _TABLE.create\nget = _TABLE.get\nlist_all = _TABLE.list_all\nset_status = _TABLE.set_status\ndelete = _TABLE.delete\n\n\nclass ReportLocked(ContentError):\n    \"\"\"A published Report, and the user is not a Director.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef can_edit(report: sqlite3.Row, user: sqlite3.Row) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"The lockdown rule. `update` applies the same rule in its UPDATE.\"\"\"\n    return report[\"status\"] == \"draft\" or is_director(user)\n\n\ndef update(report_id: int, title: str, slug: str, body: str, *,\n           user: sqlite3.Row) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Edit title, slug, and body on behalf of `user`. False if there is no\n    such Report; ReportLocked if it is published and `user` is not a Director.\"\"\"\n    if _TABLE.update(report_id, title, slug, body, drafts_only=not is_director(user)):\n        return True\n    if get(report_id) is not None:\n        raise ReportLocked(\"Only the Director can edit a published Report.\")\n    return False\n\n\ndef list_published() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"The public-facing query: published Reports only, by title.\"\"\"\n    return _TABLE.list_published(\"c.title, c.id\")\n"}},"apiBlockIndex":4,"requestId":"req_011CfWcDWKWqYVE5iYbZZLkG","type":"assistant","uuid":"402f0166-0411-48f7-b787-416c19910d32","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:15:00.664Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"high","perTurnEffort":"high","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

Once a Report is\npublished only a Director may change it: `update` enforces that in the same\nstatement that writes, and the routes make publish, unpublish, and delete\nDirector only. Editing never changes the author or created_at.\n\nPublic-facing code reads Reports only through `list_published`, so a draft\ncannot leak through it.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom app.content import ContentError, duplicate_slug_error, validate\n\n_SELECT = (\"SELECT r.*, u.name AS author_name FROM reports r\"\n           \" JOIN users u ON u.id = r.author_id\")\n\n\nclass ReportLocked(ContentError):\n    \"\"\"A published Report, and the editor is not a Director.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef can_edit(report: sqlite3.Row, user: sqlite3.Row) -> bool:\n    return report[\"status\"] == \"draft\" or user[\"role\"] == \"admin\"\n\n\ndef create(title: str, slug: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Create a draft Report.\"\"\"\n    title, slug, body = validate(title, slug, body)\n    try:\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            cursor = conn.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO reports (title, slug, body, author_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (title, slug, body, author_id),\n            )\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        raise duplicate_slug_error(exc, \"reports\", \"Report\", slug) from None\n    return cursor.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update(report_id: int, title: str, slug: str, body: str, *,\n           editor: sqlite3.Row) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Edit title, slug, and body. The author, created_at, and status are kept.\n    False if there is no such Report; ReportLocked if it is published and the\n    editor is not a Director.\"\"\"\n    title, slug, body = validate(title, slug, body)\n    is_director = editor[\"role\"] == \"admin\"\n    try:\n        with db.connect() as conn:\n            changed = conn.execute(\n                \"UPDATE reports SET title = ?, slug = ?, body = ?,\"\n                \" updated_at = datetime('now')\"\n                \" WHERE id = ? AND (status = 'draft' OR ?)\",\n                (title, slug, body, report_id, is_director),\n            ).rowcount == 1\n            if not changed and conn.execute(\n                    \"SELECT 1 FROM reports WHERE id = ?\", (report_id,)).fetchone():\n                raise ReportLocked(\"Only the Director can edit a published Report.\")\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        raise duplicate_slug_error(exc, \"reports\", \"Report\", slug) from None\n    return changed\n\n\ndef get(report_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} WHERE r.id = ?\", (report_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_all() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} ORDER BY r.updated_at DESC, r.id DESC\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef set_status(report_id: int, status: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Publish or unpublish (Director only; the routes check). False if there is\n    no such Report.\"\"\"\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE reports SET status = ?, updated_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ?\",\n            (status, report_id),\n        ).rowcount == 1\n\n\ndef delete(report_id: int) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Director only; the routes check. False if there is no such Report.\"\"\"\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM reports WHERE id = ?\", (report_id,)).rowcount == 1\n\n\ndef list_published() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"The public-facing query: published Reports only, by title.\"\"\"\n    with db.connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            f\"{_SELECT} WHERE r.status = 'published' ORDER BY r.title, r.id\"\n        ).fetchall()\n","userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"402f0166-0411-48f7-b787-416c19910d32","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"dee891e2-30dc-4c5b-9b20-aeea6ef2b2ee","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWcLpwdcU99aNUTxoJYA","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012ABz49YpcfSSRhU6W8qawA","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"echo \"BASE_URL=${ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL:-unset}\" && gh issue comment 5 --body-file - <<'EOF'\n## /code-review findings for T04 and how each was resolved\n\nI ran a two-axis review (Standards and Spec) of the T04 diff against `4b4d114`.\n\n### Standards: no hard violations\nEvery new POST requires CSRF. Publish, unpublish and delete are Director-only through `require_director`. The published-Report lock is enforced in code, not just hidden in the UI. Public code reads Reports only through `list_published`. No new dependencies.\n\n| Finding | Resolution |\n|---|---|\n| `content.py` and `db.py` cited \"ADR-003's export-path namespacing\", but ADR-003 covers publish rights only and no ADR mentions slugs | **Fixed**: they now cite spec #1, *Publish gate & export paths*. (Ticket #5's own text has the same wrong citation; flagged here and not edited.) |\n| `update(..., editor=...)` used \"editor\", a CONTEXT.md *Avoid* word | **Fixed**: renamed to `user` |\n| Duplicated Code: `reports.py` repeated `data_bites.py`'s CRUD line for line. This is the third copy the T03 review said to revisit. | **Fixed**: one `ContentTable(table, noun)` in `app/content.py` holds create/get/list/update/set_status/delete/list_published. Each type's module keeps only its own rules (Reports: `can_edit`, `ReportLocked`, the drafts-only update). This also fixes the loose `(table, noun)` strings. |\n| `user[\"role\"] == \"admin\"` repeated in Python | **Fixed**: `users.is_director()`, used by `require_director` and `reports` |\n| Lock rule written twice (`can_edit` and the SQL) | **Kept, deliberate**: the route checks first so the answer is 403; the SQL is the race-proof guard. Each place now says so in a comment. |\n| `public/home.html` duplicated the list loop; `items` vs `report_items` naming | **Fixed**: a shared `item_list` macro, and `data_bite_items` |\n| Route helpers (`_list_page`, `_edit_page`, `_get_or_404`) and the `form={...}` dict mirror Data Bites | **Kept**: the routes differ where it matters (permissions, templates); a shared route factory would hide the access rules |\n| Template role checks (`user.role == \"admin\"`) | **Kept**: matches T02/T03 templates |\n| Import order differed between the two route files | **Fixed** |\n\n### Spec: all acceptance criteria met\n\n| Finding | Resolution |\n|---|---|\n| **An invalid edit to a published Report got 400, not 403** (`validate()` ran before the lock check) | **Fixed**: the route checks `can_edit` before validating. New test: an empty body, bad slug, or empty title from an Analyst on a published Report is 403. |\n| Shift-continuity test checked the author only, not `created_at`/`updated_at` | **Fixed**: the test backdates the Report and asserts created_at is kept and updated_at changes (`backdate(table, id)` moved to `conftest.py`) |\n| Draft-leak test checks the live `/` only | **Accepted**: `/` is the only public page until T07/T08, which must re-test it |\n| Scope: T03 code refactored inside T04; a read-only Report view for Analysts; a \"Reports\" heading on `/` | **Kept**: the refactor is what the T03 review asked for. The read-only view is the GET side of the lockdown. The heading keeps Reports distinct from Data Bites until T07 builds the real nav. |\n| Publish, unpublish and delete buttons appear only on the list page, not the edit page | **Deferred to T06** (admin console UX); all actions work and are tested |\n\nTests: `tests/test_t04_reports.py` has 28 tests, and T00–T04 total 103 passed. (The 14 failures in `test_ctx_guard.py` and `test_usage_report.py` come from unimplemented template scripts and fail on `HEAD` too.) I mutation-checked three guards: removing the SQL lock, dropping `require_director` from unpublish, and removing the route's pre-validation `can_edit` check. Each made at least one test fail.\nEOF","description":"Post review findings comment on issue #5"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1112,"cache_read_input_tokens":112744,"output_tokens":2014,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":451},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1112,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":2014,"cache_read_input_tokens":112744,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1112,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1112},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_012ABz49YpcfSSRhU6W8qawA":{"command":"cd /Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms && echo \"BASE_URL=${ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL:-unset}\" && gh issue comment 5 --body-file - <<'EOF'\n## /code-review findings for T04 and how each was resolved\n\nI ran a two-axis review (Standards and Spec) of the T04 diff against `4b4d114`.\n\n### Standards: no hard violations\nEvery new POST requires CSRF. Each place now says so in a comment. |\n| `public/home.html` duplicated the list loop; `items` vs `report_items` naming | **Fixed**: a shared `item_list` macro, and `data_bite_items` |\n| Route helpers (`_list_page`, `_edit_page`, `_get_or_404`) and the `form={...}` dict mirror Data Bites | **Kept**: the routes differ where it matters (permissions, templates); a shared route factory would hide the access rules |\n| Template role checks (`user.role == \"admin\"`) | **Kept**: matches T02/T03 templates |\n| Import order differed between the two route files | **Fixed** |\n\n### Spec: all acceptance criteria met\n\n| Finding | Resolution |\n|---|---|\n| **An invalid edit to a published Report got 400, not 403** (`validate()` ran before the lock check) | **Fixed**: the route checks `can_edit` before validating. New test: an empty body, bad slug, or empty title from an Analyst on a published Report is 403. |\n| Shift-continuity test checked the author only, not `created_at`/`updated_at` | **Fixed**: the test backdates the Report and asserts created_at is kept and updated_at changes (`backdate(table, id)` moved to `conftest.py`) |\n| Draft-leak test checks the live `/` only | **Accepted**: `/` is the only public page until T07/T08, which must re-test it |\n| Scope: T03 code refactored inside T04; a read-only Report view for Analysts; a \"Reports\" heading on `/` | **Kept**: the refactor is what the T03 review asked for. The read-only view is the GET side of the lockdown. The heading keeps Reports distinct from Data Bites until T07 builds the real nav. |\n| Publish, unpublish and delete buttons appear only on the list page, not the edit page | **Deferred to T06** (admin console UX); all actions work and are tested |\n\nTests: `tests/test_t04_reports.py` has 28 tests, and T00–T04 total 103 passed. (The 14 failures in `test_ctx_guard.py` and `test_usage_report.py` come from unimplemented template scripts and fail on `HEAD` too.) I mutation-checked three guards: removing the SQL lock, dropping `require_director` from unpublish, and removing the route's pre-validation `can_edit` check. Each made at least one test fail.\nEOF","description":"Post review findings comment on issue #5"}},"wireIngestContext":{"toolu_012ABz49YpcfSSRhU6W8qawA":{"cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms"}},"apiBlockIndex":2,"requestId":"req_011CfWcLpT7n3adt8ykg3iKg","type":"assistant","uuid":"e53920ab-a16d-420d-b189-8399b75f700e","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:16:25.542Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"high","perTurnEffort":"high","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

ed3-5a716d1febd7","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:16:39.474Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"high","perTurnEffort":"high","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"9266e441-8c28-4f02-8ed3-5a716d1febd7","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWcNj2VB1F8ZQiMCbdrw","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"T04 (Reports) is done and committed to `main` as `390d40d`. The review findings, with how each was resolved, are posted on [issue #5](https://github.com/DavelleAT/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/5#issuecomment-5879738905).\n\n**What it does:**\n- **Analysts** can create a Report (always saved as a draft) and edit any draft Report, including one another Analyst started. The original author and creation time are kept.\n- **Director only:** publishing, unpublishing and deleting a Report. Once a Report is published, an Analyst's edit gets a 403 and they see it read-only.\n- **Slugs:** a second Report with the same slug is rejected with an error. A Data Bite can reuse a Report's slug.\n- **Public side:** only published Report titles appear on the public home page. Real pages and navigation come with T07.\n\n**Two review findings I fixed:**\n- **Bug:** an Analyst sending an invalid edit to a published Report got a 400 instead of 403. The permission check now runs before validation, and there's a test for it.\n- **Duplicated code:** this was the third copy the T03 review said to revisit. The save/list/publish/delete code that Data Bites and Reports share now lives in `app/content.py`, and each content type keeps only its own permission rules.\n\n**Tests:** `tests/test_t04_reports.py` has 28 tests, and all 103 app tests pass. I also broke each lockdown check on purpose, and each time at least one test failed.\n\n**Things to know:**\n- **14 other tests fail:** `test_ctx_guard.py` and `test_usage_report.py` fail on `HEAD` too, because those scripts are unimplemented stubs.\n- **No type checking:** the project has no type checker installed, so I only confirmed the code compiles.\n- **Wrong ADR citation:** issue #5 says the slug rule comes from \"ADR-003's export-path namespacing\", but ADR-003 doesn't cover slugs. I fixed the citations in the code and left the issue text for you to edit.\n- **Deferred to T06:** publish, unpublish and delete buttons appear only on the Reports list page, not the edit page.\n- **Not in the commit:** `notes/usage-ledger.csv` and the three new transcripts are still uncommitted.\n- **Issues still open:** nothing is pushed yet, so #2–#5 won't close until you push `main`."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1167,"cache_read_input_tokens":115949,"output_tokens":828,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":33},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1167,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":828,"cache_read_input_tokens":115949,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1167,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1167},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfWcNiWEK81dxrKUGiJyW","type":"assistant","uuid":"841cba17-efdf-40cf-99cc-47e1941339f5","timestamp":"2026-09-28T22:16:40.101Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"high","perTurnEffort":"high","session_id":"fc138a42-1eee-4017-bff2-c6a77302f820","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/davelleat/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"7d2512d4-d607-45c5-8b3b-db53089486cb","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
